 import java.io.*;
import java.util.Scanner;

public  class SP_ExcepTest2
{
 public  static  void main(String args[])
{
 double a[] = new  double [2];
 // a[1]=1; a[2]=3.2;

 try {
          System.out.println("Access element three :" + a[3]);
      }
 catch(ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException e)
     {
         System.out.println("Exception thrown  :" + e);
      }
 finally
       {
         a[0] = 6;
         System.out.println("First element value: " + a[0]);
         System.out.println("The finally statement is executed");
      }
   }
}

Program 1.9.1 SRP_ReadExcel.java

import java.io.File;
import java.io.IOException;

import jxl.Cell;
import jxl.CellType;
import jxl.Sheet;
import jxl.Workbook;
import jxl.read.biff.BiffException;

public  class SRP_ReadExcel
{

 private String inputFile;

 public  void setInputFile(String inputFile)
    {
 this.inputFile = inputFile;
    }
// function to read data from excel convert data from string to double & save in array
// x[][] which is returned

 public  double [][] read() throws IOException
    {
File inputWorkbook = new File(inputFile);
        Workbook w;
 double X[][]= new  double [300][10];
 // array to save excel data aribiatary 300,10 data size taken
 // it should be more than actual data size in excel

 try {
             w = Workbook.getWorkbook(inputWorkbook);
 // Get the first sheet
            Sheet sheet = w.getSheet(0);
 // Loop over first 10 column and lines
 int n=sheet.getRows();
 int  m=sheet.getColumns();
 //  double X[][]= new double [n][m];
 double Y[] = new  double [n];

 for (int i = 1; i < sheet.getRows(); i++)
            {
 for (int j = 0; j <sheet.getColumns() ; j++)

                {
                    Cell cell = sheet.getCell(j, i);
                    CellType type = cell.getType();

 if (type == CellType.LABEL)
                     {
                     X[i][j] = Double.valueOf( cell.getContents());

 // System.out.print(" " + X[i][j] );
                      } 

 if (type == CellType.NUMBER)
                     {
                    X[i][j]= Double.valueOf( cell.getContents());
 // System.out.print(" "+ X[i][j] );
                     }                            

                 }                                                                    
            }

        }

 catch (BiffException e)
         {
            e.printStackTrace();
         }

 return X ;
     }                  

 public  static  void main(String[] args) throws IOException
    {
SRP_ReadExcel test = new SRP_ReadExcel();
 // File location
        test.setInputFile("E:/Java_Engg_Problem/data.xls");

 double xx[][]= test.read();
 // save the return data from fn read to array xx[][]

 int n =xx.length; // no of rows of array xx[][]
 int m= xx[5].length;// no of columns  of array [][]

          System.out.println("n,m="+n+" "+m );

 int i,j;
 // declare array              
 double X[][]=new  double [300][10];
 int mk,nk;
 // no of actual row & colums where data are there in X[][]
 // array xx[][] might have more rows & columns than actual data are there
 //that X[][] is  subset of xx[][]
          nk=0;mk=0;

 for (  i = 1; i <xx.length ; i++)
            {
                  j=0;   
 if (xx[i][j]==0.0)break;// where no data are there

mk=0 ; // make it zero before begining column loop

 for (  j = 0; j <xx[6].length; j++)

                {
 if (xx[i][j]!=0.0) // only non zero data
                {
 //  X[nk][mk] new set of non zero datastart with X[0][0]
                  X[nk][mk]=xx[i][j];
                  System.out.print(" " + X[nk][mk] );
                  mk=mk+1;// column increament
                 }

               }
nk=nk+1; //row increament          
               System.out.println();

             }                                                                      

           System.out.println(" nk, mk=" +nk+" "+mk);
        } 

}

12.2 Chapter 2 Numerical Methods in Engineering

  Program 2.3.1 SP_NumericalDifferentiation.java

 import java.util.Scanner;
 import java.io.*;
 import java.math.*;

 public  class SP_NumericalDifferentiation
    {            
 double d1[]= new  double[10];
 double d2[]= new  double[10];
 double d3[]= new  double[10];
 double d4[]= new  double[10];
 double d5[]= new  double[10];
 double y[]=new  double [10];
 double df; double dff;
 double yy ;

 public  double fn( double xx)
     {
 double A,B,C,D,h;
       A=2;  B=1;  C=2.5;  D=3;

 double yy=A*xx + B*Math.pow(xx,2) + C*Math.log(xx) +D ;
 return yy ;
    }

public  double dfn( double []y,double h)
{
 double df;

    d1[1]= ((y[2]) -(y[1]));
      d1[2]= ((y[3]) -(y[2]));
      d1[3]= ((y[4]) -(y[3]));
     d1[4]= ((y[5]) -(y[4]));
      d1[5]=((y[6]) -(y[5]));

     d2[1]=d1[2]-d1[1];
      d2[2]=d1[3]-d1[2];
      d2[3]=d1[4]-d1[3];
      d2[4]=d1[5]-d1[4];

     d3[1]=d2[2]-d2[1];
     d3[2]=d2[3]-d2[2];
      d3[3]=d2[4]-d2[3];

     d4[1]=d3[2]-d3[1];
     d4[2]=d3[3]-d3[2];
     d5[1]=d4[2]-d4[1];

  df=(d1[1]-d2[1]/2 +d3[1]/3 -d4[1]/4 + d5[1])/h;

 return df ;
  }

 public  double ddfn( double []y,double h)
{
 double ddf;
     d1[1]= ((y[2]) -(y[1]));
      d1[2]= ((y[3]) -(y[2]));
      d1[3]= ((y[4]) -(y[3]));
     d1[4]= ((y[5]) -(y[4]));
      d1[5]=((y[6]) -(y[5]));

     d2[1]=d1[2]-d1[1];
      d2[2]=d1[3]-d1[2];
      d2[3]=d1[4]-d1[3];
      d2[4]=d1[5]-d1[4];
     d3[1]=d2[2]-d2[1];
     d3[2]=d2[3]-d2[2];
      d3[3]=d2[4]-d2[3];
     d4[1]=d3[2]-d3[1];
     d4[2]=d3[3]-d3[2];
     d5[1]=d4[2]-d4[1];

    ddf=(d2[1]-d3[1] + (11/12)*d4[1] -(5/6)*d5[1])/(Math.pow(h,2));

 return ddf ;
  }
 public  static  void main(String[] args)
        {
 double x[]= new  double[150];
 double y[]= new  double[150];
 int i,j;
SP_NumericalDifferentiation nd= new     

       SP_NumericalDifferentiation();

Scanner inpData = new Scanner(System.in);

      System.out.println("Enter the value of x");
 double   xx = inpData.nextDouble();

    System.out.println("Enter the increament h for finite diff method");
 double  h = inpData.nextDouble();

    i=1;
    x[i]=xx;
 do
   {  
   y[i]=nd.fn(x[i]);
   i=i+1;
   x[i]=x[i-1] +h;
// System.out.print ("x= "+ x[i]+ "fn= "+y[i]);

} while (x[i]<=x[1]+7*h);
   System.out.println();

 double df=nd.dfn(y,h);
 double ddf=nd.ddfn(y,h);

 double value=2+2*xx + 2.5/xx;// real value 1st dervative
 double value2=2 - 2.5/(xx*xx);// real value 2nd dervative

//Display Results

  System.out.println("1st Derivative=" +df +"  Real value=" +value);
System.out.println("2nd Derivative=" +ddf+"  Real value=" +value2 );
}                  
    }

Program 2.4.1 SP_NewtonRaphson

    import java.util.Scanner;
 import java.io.*;
 import java.math.*;

 /* y= Ax+Bx2 + Clogx + D */

 public  class SP_NewtonRaphson
    {            
 double d1[]= new  double[10];
 double d2[]= new  double[10];
 double d3[]= new  double[10];
 double d4[]= new  double[10];
 double d5[]= new  double[10];
 double y[]=new  double [10];
 double x[]= new  double[150];

 double df; double dff;
 double yy ;

 public  double fn( double xx)
     {
 double A,B,C,D,h;

       A=-4;  B=1;  C=2 ;  D=3.4;

 double yy=A*xx + B*Math.pow(xx,2) + C*Math.log(xx) +D ;
 // double yy= Math.pow(xx,2)-4*xx + 4 ;
 return yy ;
    } 

public  double dfn( double xx,double h)
{
 double df;
 int i=1;
    x[i]=xx;

 do
   {

   y[i]= fn(x[i]);
   i=i+1;
   x[i]=x[i-1] +h;

} while (x[i]<=x[1]+7*h);

    d1[1]= ((y[2]) -(y[1]));
      d1[2]= ((y[3]) -(y[2]));
      d1[3]= ((y[4]) -(y[3]));
     d1[4]= ((y[5]) -(y[4]));
      d1[5]=((y[6]) -(y[5]));

     d2[1]=d1[2]-d1[1];
      d2[2]=d1[3]-d1[2];
      d2[3]=d1[4]-d1[3];
      d2[4]=d1[5]-d1[4];

     d3[1]=d2[2]-d2[1];
     d3[2]=d2[3]-d2[2];
      d3[3]=d2[4]-d2[3];

     d4[1]=d3[2]-d3[1];
     d4[2]=d3[3]-d3[2];
     d5[1]=d4[2]-d4[1];

  df=(d1[1]-d2[1]/2 +d3[1]/3 -d4[1]/4 + d5[1])/h;

 return df ;
  }
 public  static  void main(String[] args)
        {
 int i,j;
SP_NewtonRaphson nd= new SP_NewtonRaphson();

Scanner inpData = new Scanner(System.in);

            System.out.println("Enter an approximate value  of x");
 double   xx = inpData.nextDouble();

           System.out.println("Enter the increament h for finite diff

          method");
 double  h = inpData.nextDouble();

 double xnew;
    xnew=xx;

 for (i=1;i<8 ;i++)
  {
    xnew= xx- nd.fn(xx)/nd.dfn(xx,h);
    xx=xnew;
     System.out.println("x=" +xnew);
   }         

     }                  
  }

Program 2.4.3 SP_Gauss_Elimination.java

import java.util.Scanner;

public  class SP_Gauss_Elimination
{
public  static  void main(String args[])

{
int i=1,j=1,itr=1;
 double  sum=0,temp=0,  tol=0.001,max=0,error=0;

Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);

    System.out.println("Enter the number of variables: ");
 int n = input.nextInt();

  System.out.println("Enter the coefficients of a11x1 + a12x2 + a13x3 + ... =0 for each equations");
 double a[][]   = new  double[n+1][n+2];

 //input
 for (i=1; i<n+1; i++)
   {
 for(j=1; j<n+2; j++)
      {
          a[i][j] = input.nextDouble();
      }     
   }
 //Matrix representation
System.out.println("The coefficients you entered are:");

 for (i=1; i<n+1; i++)
      {
 for(j=1; j<n+2; j++)
            {
             System.out.print(" " +a[i][j]);
             }                    
              System.out.println();

       }         

       System.out.println();         
 double x[]= new  double [n+1];

 for (i=1; i<n+1; i++)
           {
             x[i]=0.0;
           }                        

 for(itr=1;itr<200; itr++)
   {
        max=0;
 for(i=1; i<(n+1);i++)
      {
         sum=0;
 for(j=1;j<(n+1);j++)
 if(i!=j)
         {
            sum= sum + a[i][j]*x[j];
         }
         temp=(a[i][n+1] - sum )/a[i][i];
         error=((x[i]-temp));
 if (error>max)
            max=error;
 else
            max=-error;
            x[i]=temp;             
      }
 if(max<tol)
 break;
      }
 for (i=1; i<n+1; i++)
      {                             
        System.out.print(" x"+i + x[i]);
         System.out.println();
       }     
   }
}

Program  2.4.4 SP_Gauss.java

import java.io.*;
import java.util.Scanner;

class SP_Gauss
{
public  static  double[] gauss(double a[][],double b[])
{
 int n=b.length;
 double carpan=0;
double toplam=0;
double x[]=new  double[n];
//gauss eleme
int i,j,k;
for(k=0;k<(n-1);k++)
{
for(i=k+1;i<n;i++)
{ carpan=a[i][k]/a[k][k];
a[i][k]=0;
for(j=k+1;j<n;j++)
{ a[i][j]-=carpan*a[k][j]; }

b[i] =b[i] -carpan*b[k];
}
}
//back substituting
x[n-1]=b[n-1]/a[n-1][n-1];
for(i=n-2;i>=0;i--)
{
toplam=0;
for(j=i+1;j<n;j++)
{ toplam+=a[i][j]*x[j];}
x[i]=(b[i]-toplam)/a[i][i];
}
return x;
}

public  static  void main(String args[])

{
SP_Gauss g = new SP_Gauss();
  System.out.println("Enter the number of variables: ");

Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);

int n = input.nextInt();

double a[][]   = new  double[n][n];
 double b[]  = new  double[n];
 //input  
System.out.println("Enter the matrix A & B in AX=B: ");

 for (int i=0; i<n; i++)
   {
 for(int j=0; j<n; j++)
      {
          a[i][j] = input.nextDouble();
      }
System.out.println ( " Enter B part");
      b[i]=input.nextDouble();
   }

 double x[]=g.gauss(a,b);
 for (int i=0; i<x.length; i++)
  {
  System.out.print("Results x values " +x[i] );
  }
    }
}

import java.util.Scanner;

public  class SP_LUDecomposition
{
 public  static  void main(String args[])
    {
Scanner sc = new Scanner(System.in);
System.out.println("Enter the dimension of the matrix:");

 int n = sc.nextInt();
 double [][]mat = new  double[n][n];

System.out.println("Enter the dimension of the matrix A ::B ");
 for(int i=0; i<n; i++)

 for(int j=0; j<n; j++)

               mat[i][j] = sc.nextDouble();

 if(n==2)

        {
 double [][]l = new  double[n][n];

            l[0][0] = l[1][1] = 1;
            l[0][1] = 0;

 double [][]u = new  double[n][n];

            u[1][0] = 0;
            u[0][0] = mat[0][0];
            u[0][1] = mat[0][1];

            l[1][0] = mat[1][0]/mat[0][0];

            u[1][1] = mat[1][1] - (l[1][0]*u[0][1]);  

 //mat[2][2]-(mat[2][1]*mat[1][2]/mat[1][1]);

            System.out.println("The L Component is:");

 for(int i=0; i<n; i++)
            {
 for(int j=0; j<n; j++)

                    System.out.print(" "+l[i][j]);
                System.out.println();
            }

            System.out.println("The U Component is:");

 for(int i=0; i<n; i++)
            {
 for(int j=0; j<n; j++)

                    System.out.print(" "+u[i][j]);
                System.out.println();
            }
        }
 if(n==3)
        {
 double [][]l = new  double[n][n];
            l[0][0] = l[1][1] = l[2][2] = 1;
            l[0][1] = l[0][2] = l[1][2] = 0;

 double [][]u = new  double[n][n];

            u[1][0] = u[2][0] = u[2][1] = 0;

             u[0][0] = mat[0][0];

            u[0][1] = mat[0][1];

            u[0][2] = mat[0][2];

            l[1][0] = mat[1][0]/mat[0][0];

            u[1][1] = mat[1][1] - (l[1][0]*u[0][1]);  

            u[1][2] = mat[1][2] - (l[1][0]*u[0][2]);

             l[2][0] = mat[2][0]/u[0][0];

            l[2][1] = (mat[2][1] - l[2][1]*u[0][1])/u[1][1];

            u[2][2] = mat[2][2] - (l[2][0]*u[0][2]) - (l[2][1]*u[1][2]);

             System.out.println("The L Component is:");

 for(int i=0; i<n; i++)
            {
 for(int j=0; j<n; j++)

                   System.out.print(" "+l[i][j]);
                  System.out.println();
            }

            System.out.println("The U Component is:");

 for(int i=0; i<n; i++)
            {
 for(int j=0; j<n; j++)
                    System.out.print(" "+u[i][j]);
                System.out.println();
            }
        }
        sc.close();
    }
}

The program code is given in the Appendix

 //This is a sample program to solve the linear equations.

 import java.util.Scanner;

 public  class SP_LinearMultiVariables
    {

 public  static  void main(String args[])
        {
 char []var = {'x', 'y', 'z', 'w'};

            System.out.println("Enter the number of variables in the

                                      equations: ");
Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);

 int n = input.nextInt();

            System.out.println("Enter the coefficients of each variable

                                        for each equations");

            System.out.println("ax + by + cz + ... = d");

 double [][]mat = new  double[n][n];
 double [][]constants = new  double[n][1];

 //input
 for(int i=0; i<n; i++)
            {
 for(int j=0; j<n; j++)
                {
                    mat[i][j] = input.nextDouble();
                }
                constants[i][0] = input.nextDouble();
            }

 //Matrix representation

 for(int i=0; i<n; i++)
            {
 for(int j=0; j<n; j++)
                {
                   System.out.print(" "+mat[i][j]);
                }
                System.out.print("  "+ var[i]);
                System.out.print("  =  "+ constants[i][0]);
               System.out.println();
            }

 //inverse of matrix mat[][]

 double inverted_mat[][] = invert(mat);

            System.out.println("The inverse is: ");

 for (int i=0; i<n; ++i)
            {
 for (int j=0; j<n; ++j)

                {
                    System.out.print(inverted_mat[i][j]+"  ");
                }

                System.out.println();
            }

 //Multiplication of mat inverse and constants

 double result[][] = new  double[n][1];

 for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
            {
 for (int j = 0; j < 1; j++)
                {
 for (int k = 0; k < n; k++)
                    {   
                        result[i][j] = result[i][j] + inverted_mat[i][k] * constants[k][j];
                    }
                }
            }

            System.out.println("the unknwon variable are:");

 for(int i=0; i<n; i++)
            {
System.out.println(result[i][0] + " ");
            }
            input.close(); 
        }

 public  static  double[][] invert(double a[][])
        {
 int n = a.length;
 double x[][] = new  double[n][n];

 double b[][] = new  double[n][n];
 int index[] = new  int[n];
 for (int i=0; i<n; ++i)
                b[i][i] = 1;

 // Transform the matrix into an upper triangle

            gaussian(a, index);

 // Update the matrix b[i][j] with the ratios stored

 for (int i=0; i<n-1; ++i)

 for (int j=i+1; j<n; ++j)

 for (int k=0; k<n; ++k)

                 b[index[j]][k]-= a[index[j]][i]*b[index[i]][k];

 // Perform backward substitutions

 for (int i=0; i<n; ++i)
            {
                x[n-1][i] = b[index[n-1]][i]/a[index[n-1]][n-1];
 for (int j=n-2; j>=0; --j)
                {
                    x[j][i] = b[index[j]][i];

 for (int k=j+1; k<n; ++k)
                    {
                        x[j][i] -= a[index[j]][k]*x[k][i];
                    }
                    x[j][i] /= a[index[j]][j];
               }
            }
 return x;
                     } 

 // Method to carry out the partial-pivoting Gaussian

 // elimination.  Here index[] stores pivoting order.

 public  static  void gaussian(double a[][], int index[])
        {
 int n = index.length;
 double c[] = new  double[n];   

 // Initialize the index
 for (int i=0; i<n; ++i)
                index[i] = i;

 // Find the rescaling factors, one from each row

 for (int i=0; i<n; ++i)
            {
 double c1 = 0;
 for (int j=0; j<n; ++j)

                {
 double c0 = Math.abs(a[i][j]);

 if (c0 > c1) c1 = c0;
                }
                c[i] = c1;
            }

 // Search the pivoting element from each column

 int k = 0;

 for (int j=0; j<n-1; ++j)
            {
 double pi1 = 0;

 for (int i=j; i<n; ++i)

                {
 double pi0 = Math.abs(a[index[i]][j]);
                    pi0 /= c[index[i]];
 if (pi0 > pi1)
                    {
                        pi1 = pi0;
                        k = i;
                    }
                }

 // Interchange rows according to the pivoting order

 int itmp = index[j];

                index[j] = index[k];

                index[k] = itmp;

 for (int i=j+1; i<n; ++i)

                {
 double pj = a[index[i]][j]/a[index[j]][j];

 // Record pivoting ratios below the diagonal
                    a[index[i]][j] = pj;

 // Modify other elements accordingly
 for (int l=j+1; l<n; ++l)

                        a[index[i]][l] -= pj*a[index[j]][l];
                }
            }
        }
    }

Program  2.4.7 SP_MultipleRoot_1Variable.java

 import java.util.Scanner;
 import java.io.*;
 import java.math.*;

 /* y= Ax+Bx2 + Clogx + D */

 public  class SP_MultipleRoot_1Variable
    {            
 double d1[]= new  double[10];
 double d2[]= new  double[10];
 double d3[]= new  double[10];
 double d4[]= new  double[10];
 double d5[]= new  double[10];
 double y[]=new  double [10];
 double df; double dff;
 double yy ;

 public  double fn( double xx)
     {
 double A,B,C,D,h;

       A=2;  B=1;  C=2.5;  D=3;

 double yy=A*xx + B*Math.pow(xx,2) + C*Math.log(xx) +D ;
 return yy ;
    }

public  double dfn( double []y,double h)
{
 double df;

    d1[1]= ((y[2]) -(y[1]));
      d1[2]= ((y[3]) -(y[2]));
      d1[3]= ((y[4]) -(y[3]));
     d1[4]= ((y[5]) -(y[4]));
      d1[5]=((y[6]) -(y[5]));

     d2[1]=d1[2]-d1[1];
      d2[2]=d1[3]-d1[2];
      d2[3]=d1[4]-d1[3];
      d2[4]=d1[5]-d1[4];

     d3[1]=d2[2]-d2[1];
     d3[2]=d2[3]-d2[2];
      d3[3]=d2[4]-d2[3];

     d4[1]=d3[2]-d3[1];
     d4[2]=d3[3]-d3[2];
     d5[1]=d4[2]-d4[1];

  df=(d1[1]-d2[1]/2 +d3[1]/3 -d4[1]/4 + d5[1])/h;

 return df ;
  }

 public  double ddfn( double []y,double h)
{
 double ddf;
     d1[1]= ((y[2]) -(y[1]));
      d1[2]= ((y[3]) -(y[2]));
      d1[3]= ((y[4]) -(y[3]));
     d1[4]= ((y[5]) -(y[4]));
      d1[5]=((y[6]) -(y[5]));

     d2[1]=d1[2]-d1[1];
      d2[2]=d1[3]-d1[2];
      d2[3]=d1[4]-d1[3];
      d2[4]=d1[5]-d1[4];

     d3[1]=d2[2]-d2[1];
     d3[2]=d2[3]-d2[2];
      d3[3]=d2[4]-d2[3];

     d4[1]=d3[2]-d3[1];
     d4[2]=d3[3]-d3[2];
     d5[1]=d4[2]-d4[1];

    ddf=(d2[1]-d3[1] + (11/12)*d4[1] -(5/6)*d5[1])/(Math.pow(h,2));

 return ddf ;
  }

 public  static  void main(String[] args)

        {
 double x[]= new  double[150];
 double y[]= new  double[150];
 int i,j;
 double x1,x2,xx1,xx2;
SP_MultipleRoot_1Variable nd= new SP_MultipleRoot_1Variable();

Scanner inpData = new Scanner(System.in);

      System.out.println("Enter Enter approximate root of  x");
 double   xx = inpData.nextDouble();

    System.out.println("Enter the increament h for finite diff method");
 double  h = inpData.nextDouble();

    i=1;
    x[i]=xx;
 do
   {

   y[i]=nd.fn(x[i]);
   i=i+1;
   x[i]=x[i-1] +h;
// System.out.print ("x= "+ x[i]+ "fn= "+y[i]);

} while (x[i]<=x[1]+7*h);
   System.out.println();

 double f=nd.fn(xx);
 double df=nd.dfn(y,h);
 double ddf=nd.ddfn(y,h);

      x1= xx -2*f/df;
      x2=xx - df/ddf;

 do
   {
    f=nd.fn(xx);
    df=nd.dfn(y,h);   
    ddf=nd.ddfn(y,h);
    xx1= x1 -2*f/df;
   xx2=x2 - df/ddf;
   x1=xx1;
   x2=xx2;   
i=i+1;
} while (i<=5);

  System.out.println("x1=" +x1+"  x2=" +x2);

}                  
    }

Program 2.5.1 SP_NonLinEq2Variables.java

 import java.util.Scanner;
 import java.io.*;
 import java.math.*;

 public  class SP_NonLinEq2Variables
        {
 double f,g, dfx,dgx,dfy,dgy,hx,hy,A1,B1,C1,D1,A2,B2,C2,D2,x,y;

 public  double []Compute ()
    {
Scanner inpData = new Scanner(System.in);

    A1=0.0;B1=10.0;C1=-1.0; D1=-5.0;
    A2=0.0;B2=2.0; C2=1.0;D2=-7.0;
 int i=1;

   System.out.println("Enter the increament h for finite diff method");
 double  h = inpData.nextDouble();

    System.out.println("Enter an approximate value  of X");
 double x= inpData.nextDouble();

   System.out.println("Enter an approximate value  of Y");
 double y= inpData.nextDouble();

do{   
  f=A1/y + B1*y + C1*Math.log(x) + D1;
   g=A2/y + B2*y + C2*Math.log(x) + D2;

  dfx= C1/x;
  dfy= -A1/(Math.pow(y,2)) + B1;

  dgx=C2/x;   
  dgy= -A2/(Math.pow(y,2)) + B2;

 double A[]= solve();
    hx=A[0];
    hy=A[1];
   x=x+hx;
    y=y+hy;
   i=i+1;
} while (i<=8);

 double XY[] = {x,y};
 return (XY);
  }

 public  double[] solve()
    {
    hx=(-f/dfy+g/dgy)/(dfx/dfy - dgx/dgy) ;
     hy=( -f/dfy-dfx*hx/dfy) ;

 double A[]={hx,hy};
 return (A);
    }

 public  static  void main(String args[])
  {
SP_NonLinEq2Variables   nl= new SP_NonLinEq2Variables();
 double XY[]= nl.Compute();
    System.out.println (" x="+XY[0]+"  y="+XY[1]);     
    }
  }

The program 2.6  SP_LinearRegression .java

import java.io.*;
import java.util.Scanner;

public  class SP_LinearRegression
{
 private  final  double intercept, slope;
 private  final  double r2;
 private  final  double svar0, svar1;

 public SP_LinearRegression(double[] x, double[] y)
     {
 if (x.length != y.length)
        {
 throw  new IllegalArgumentException("array lengths are not

         equal");
        }
 int n = x.length;

 double sumx = 0.0, sumy = 0.0, sumx2 = 0.0;
 for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
        {
            sumx  += x[i];
            sumx2 += x[i]*x[i];
            sumy  += y[i];
        }
 double xbar = sumx / n;
 double ybar = sumy / n;

 double xxbar = 0.0, yybar = 0.0, xybar = 0.0;

 for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
        {
            xxbar += (x[i] - xbar) * (x[i] - xbar);
            yybar += (y[i] - ybar) * (y[i] - ybar);
            xybar += (x[i] - xbar) * (y[i] - ybar);
        }
        slope  = xybar / xxbar;
        intercept = ybar - slope * xbar;

 // more statistical analysis
 double rss = 0.0; // residual sum of squares
 double ssr = 0.0; // regression sum of squares
 for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
        {
 double fit = slope*x[i] + intercept;
            rss += (fit - y[i]) * (fit - y[i]);
            ssr += (fit - ybar) * (fit - ybar);
        }

 int degreesOfFreedom = n-2;
        r2    = ssr / yybar;
 double svar  = rss / degreesOfFreedom;
        svar1 = svar / xxbar;
        svar0 = svar/n + xbar*xbar*svar1;
    }

 public  double intercept()
    {
 return intercept;
    }

 public  double slope()
     {
 return slope;
     }

 public  double R2()
    {
 return r2;
     }

 public  double interceptStdErr()
    {
 return Math.sqrt(svar0);
    }

 public  double slopeStdErr()
    {
 return Math.sqrt(svar1);
    }

 public  double predict(double x)
    {
 return slope*x + intercept;
    }

 public String toString()
    {
StringBuilder s = new StringBuilder();
        s.append(String.format("%.2f n + %.2f", slope(), intercept()));
        s.append("  (R^2 = " + String.format("%.3f", R2()) + ")");
 return s.toString();
    }

 public  static  void main(String args[])

   {
 int n  ;
Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);

      System.out.print("Enter number of set data ");

     n = input.nextInt();

 double x[]= new  double[n];
 double y[]= new  double[n];        

    System.out.println("Enter x & y for " +n + " no of data");

 for(int i=0;i<n;i++)
    {//for reading array
       x[i]=input.nextDouble();
       y[i]=input.nextDouble();
    }
LinearRegression lnrg = new LinearRegression ( x,y) ;

 double sp= lnrg.slope() ;
 double inp= lnrg.intercept() ;
 double reg=lnrg.R2();

        System.out.println("slope= " +sp );
        System.out.println("intercept= " +inp );
        System.out.println("R= " +reg );
     }
   }

12.4 Chapter 4 Process Optimization and Linear Programming

Program 4.3.3.3   SRP_Simplex.java.java

import java.io.*;
 import java.util.Scanner;
 import java.math.*;

public  class SRP_Simplex {

private  double[][] tableaux; // tableaux
private  int numberOfConstraints; // number of constraints
private  int numberOfOriginalVariables; // number of original variables

private  boolean maximizeOrMinimize;

private  static  final  boolean MAXIMIZE = true;
private  static  final  boolean MINIMIZE = false;

private  int[] basis; // basis[i] = basic variable corresponding to row i

 public SRP_Simplex(double[][] tableaux, int numberOfConstraint,
 int numberOfOriginalVariable, boolean maximizeOrMinimize)
   {
 this.maximizeOrMinimize = maximizeOrMinimize;
 this.numberOfConstraints = numberOfConstraint;
 this.numberOfOriginalVariables = numberOfOriginalVariable;
 this.tableaux = tableaux;

basis = new  int[numberOfConstraints];
 for (int i = 0; i < numberOfConstraints; i++)
      basis[i] = numberOfOriginalVariables + i;

      solve();
  }

// run simplex algorithm starting from initial BFS

 private  void solve()
  {
 while (true) {

   show();
 int q = 0;
 // find entering column q
 if (maximizeOrMinimize)
      {
      q = dantzig();
       }
 else
    {
      q = dantzigNegative();
     }
 if (q == -1)
 break; // optimal

 // find leaving row p
 int p = minRatioRule(q);
 if (p == -1)
 throw  new ArithmeticException("Linear program is unbounded");

 // pivot
   pivot(p, q);

 // update basis
   basis[p] = q;

  }
}
// index of a non-basic column with most positive cost
private  int dantzig()
{
 int q = 0;
 for (int j = 1; j < numberOfConstraints + numberOfOriginalVariables; j++)
 if (tableaux[numberOfConstraints][j] > tableaux[numberOfConstraints][q])
    q = j;

 if (tableaux[numberOfConstraints][q] <= 0)
 return -1; // optimal
 else
 return q;
}
// index of a non-basic column with most negative cost
private  int dantzigNegative() {
 int q = 0;
 for (int j = 1; j < numberOfConstraints + numberOfOriginalVariables; j++)
 if (tableaux[numberOfConstraints][j] < tableaux[numberOfConstraints][q])
    q = j;

 if (tableaux[numberOfConstraints][q] >= 0)
 return -1; // optimal
 else
 return q;
}

// find row p using min ratio rule (-1 if no such row)
private  int minRatioRule(int q) {
 int p = -1;
 for (int i = 0; i < numberOfConstraints; i++) {
 if (tableaux[i][q] <= 0)
 continue;
 else  if (p == -1)
    p = i;
 else  if ((tableaux[i][numberOfConstraints
     + numberOfOriginalVariables] / tableaux[i][q]) < (tableaux[p][numberOfConstraints
     + numberOfOriginalVariables] / tableaux[p][q]))
    p = i;
  }
 return p;
}

// pivot on entry (p, q) using Gauss-Jordan elimination
private  void pivot(int p, int q) {

 // everything but row p and column q
 for (int i = 0; i <= numberOfConstraints; i++)
 for (int j = 0; j <= numberOfConstraints
     + numberOfOriginalVariables; j++)
 if (i != p && j != q)
     tableaux[i][j] -= tableaux[p][j] * tableaux[i][q]
       / tableaux[p][q];

 // zero out column q
 for (int i = 0; i <= numberOfConstraints; i++)
 if (i != p)
    tableaux[i][q] = 0.0;

 // scale row p
 for (int j = 0; j <= numberOfConstraints + numberOfOriginalVariables; j++)
 if (j != q)
    tableaux[p][j] /= tableaux[p][q];
  tableaux[p][q] = 1.0;
}

// return optimal objective value
public  double value() {
 return -tableaux[numberOfConstraints][numberOfConstraints
    + numberOfOriginalVariables];
}

// return  solution vector
 public  double[] solution()
  {
 double[] x = new  double[numberOfOriginalVariables];
 for (int i = 0; i < numberOfConstraints; i++)
 if (basis[i] < numberOfOriginalVariables)
    x[basis[i]] = tableaux[i][numberOfConstraints
      + numberOfOriginalVariables];
 return x;
  }

// print tableaux
public  void show() {
  System.out.println("M = " + numberOfConstraints);
  System.out.println("N = " + numberOfOriginalVariables);
 for (int i = 0; i <= numberOfConstraints; i++) {
 for (int j = 0; j <= numberOfConstraints
     + numberOfOriginalVariables; j++) {
    System.out.printf("%7.2f ", tableaux[i][j]);
   }
   System.out.println();
  }
  System.out.println("value = " + value());
 for (int i = 0; i < numberOfConstraints; i++)
 if (basis[i] < numberOfOriginalVariables)
    System.out.println("x_"
      + basis[i]
+ " = "
      + tableaux[i][numberOfConstraints
        + numberOfOriginalVariables]);
  System.out.println();
}

// Main function ****************************************************
 public  static  void main(String[] args)
  {
InpData inp = new InpData( );
          inp.getTableaux();
System.out.println (inp.getTableaux());

SRP_Simplex simplex = new SRP_Simplex(inp.getTableaux(),
    inp.getNumberOfConstraint(),
   inp.getNumberOfOriginalVariable(), MAXIMIZE);

 double[] x = simplex.solution();
 for (int i = 0; i < x.length; i++)
   System.out.println("x[" + i + "] = " + x[i]);
  System.out.println("Solution: " + simplex.value());
  }

private  enum Constraint {
  lessThan, equal, greatherThan
}

public  static  class InpData
{
 private  double[][] a=new  double[10][10]; // tableaux
 private  int numberOfConstraints; // number of constraints
 private  int numberOfOriginalVariables; // number of original variables
 double[][] constraintLeftSide=new  double[10][10];
 double[] constraintRightSide = new  double[10];
   Constraint[] constraintOperator;
 double[] objectiveFunction= new  double[10];  

 public InpData()

{ Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);

    System.out.println("Enter No of Constrains & No of variables in Obj fn");       
    numberOfConstraints=input.nextInt();
    numberOfOriginalVariables=input.nextInt();

 //  a = new double[numberOfConstraints + 1][numberOfOriginalVariables
 //   + numberOfConstraints + 1];

     System.out.println("Enter constraintRightSide[i] coeficient");

 for (int i = 0; i < numberOfConstraints; i++)
   {
    constraintRightSide[i]= input.nextDouble();
    a[i][numberOfConstraints + numberOfOriginalVariables] = constraintRightSide[i];
    }

    System.out.println("Enter objectivefunc coeficient");

for (int j = 0; j < numberOfOriginalVariables; j++)
     {
       objectiveFunction[j]=  input.nextDouble();
       a[numberOfConstraints][j] = objectiveFunction[j];
      }

 // initialize constraint
   System.out.println("Enter constraintLeftSide[i][j] coeficient");

 for (int i = 0; i < numberOfConstraints; i++)
   {
 for (int j = 0; j < numberOfOriginalVariables; j++)
       {
           constraintLeftSide[i][j] = input.nextDouble();
           a[i][j] = constraintLeftSide[i][j];
       }
   }
 // initialize slack variable
 int slack = 0;
    System.out.println("Enter constraintOperator  RHS -1 for greatherThan( >)+1 for Less than (<) & 0 for =");

 for (int i = 0; i < numberOfConstraints; i++)
    {
      slack=input.nextInt();
       a[i][numberOfOriginalVariables + i] = slack;
    }

  }

 public  double[][] getTableaux()
   {
 return a;
   }

 public  int getNumberOfConstraint() {
 return numberOfConstraints;
  }

 public  int getNumberOfOriginalVariable()
   {
 return numberOfOriginalVariables;
   }
}
}

Program 4.7.1   SP_LinerProg.java

/*
Linear Program . This program has been tested & O K . works on
constrains <= surplus/ slack varibles
n= Total no of varibles
m= Total no of constrains
n1= No of varibles in the objective function
n2= No of slack <  surplus > varibles
n3=No of artficial varibles due to = sign
n=n1+n2+n3 */

import java.io.*;
import java.util.Scanner;

public  class SP_LinerProg
{
 private  int i,j,k,kk,r,n,m,m1,m2,m3; // n  no of unknown, m no of

 private  float[][] a; // simplex tableau
 // i  Row No, constrain eqn. No.   ,j column varible X1,X2

 private  float[] CB =new  float[20];
 private  float[] x=new  float[20];
 private  float[] b=new  float[20];
 private  float[] s=new  float[20];
 private  float[]Z=new  float[20];
 private  float[]C=new  float[20];
 private  float[]q=new  float[20];
 private  float Cjsum ;
 private  float e;

 //  float c[20],a[20][20],x[20],b[20], s[20],Z[20],CB[20],C[20],q[20],Cjsum;

 public SP_LinerProg(int n, int m, float c[],float a[][], float b[])
    {
 // Making all CB zero

 for (i=1; i<=m; i++)
        {
           CB[i]=c[n-i+1];
 // x[i]=b[n-i+1];
        }
 // Determining Z= summation (CB[i]*a[i][j])
 do{

            Z[1]= 0 ;

 for (j=1; j<=n; j++)
         {

 for (i=1; i<=m; i++)
              {
 // cout<<"CB[i]"<<CB[i]<<"a[i][j]"<<a[i][j]<<"\n";
                 Z[j]=Z[j] + CB[i]*a[i][j];
               }

          }

 for (j=1; j<=n; j++)
         { 
            C[j]=c[j]-Z[j];
          }

 // Begin Table

System.out.println("c[j]=" );

for (j=1; j<=n; j++)
   {
   System.out.print (" "+c[j]);
   }

System.out.println();

for (j=1; j<=n; j++)
  {
System.out.print("  x"+j);
}

System.out.println();

 for (i=1; i<=m; i++)
  {  

System.out.print("x"+i+"\t");

 // cout<<"\t";
 for (j=1; j<=n; j++)
   {
System.out.print( "  "+a[i][j] );
   }
  System.out.print("  " +b[i] +"\t"+ q[i]);

    System.out.println();
  }
System.out.println("Z");
 for (j=1; j<=n; j++)
  {
  System.out.print (" "+Z[j]);
  }
System.out.println();

for (j=1; j<=n; j++)
  {
System.out.print(" "+C[j]);
  }
System.out.println();
System.out.println("CB");
for (i=1; i<=m; i++)
{
   System.out.print("  "+CB[i]);
}

System.out.println();
// end of table
kk=k;
// rr=n+1;

for (i=1; i<=n-m; i++)
{

   x[i]=b[i];

System.out.println("x"+i+"="+x[i]) ;
}  
// find key column j of max Cj ,to select key column, call that column k=j
 float max;
         max=(C[1]);

 for (j=1; j<=n; j++)
      {
 if(max<=(C[j]))
            { max=C[j]; k=j; }
      }

 // Find Key row
 // Divide  b column by corresponding a of k coloumn.

 for (i=1; i<=m; i++)
     { 
 if(a[i][k]==0) a[i][k]=e;
           q[i]=b[i]/a[i][k];

      }

 float mini;
         mini=q[1];

 for (i=1; i<=m; i++)
    {
 if(q[i]<=mini)

        {
          mini=q[i]; r=i;
        }
     }

CB[r]=c[k]; // value of CB becomes coeffient of incomoing x
       x[r]=x[k];

 float temp=a[r][k] ;

      b[r]=b[r]/a[r][k];

 for (j=1; j<=n; j++)
     {
       a[r][j]=a[r][j]/temp;   
      }

 for (i=1; i<=m; i++)
       {
 if (i!=r)
         b[i]=b[i]-b[r]*a[i][k];

        }
 for (i=1; i<=m; i++)
      {
 if (i!=r)
            {temp=a[i][k];
 for (j=1; j<=n; j++)
               {    

                  a[i][j]=a[i][j]-temp*a[r][j];
                }
             }

       }

 float Zmax=0;
 for (j=1; j<=n-m; j++) // 4 no of slack + artificial variables
       {
           Zmax=Zmax + c[j]*x[j];
       }
             Cjsum=0;
 for (j=1; j<=n; j++)
         {
            Cjsum = Cjsum + C[j] ;
         }

} while( Cjsum<=0);

}
 public  static  void main(String args[])
      {
 int n,m  ;
Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);

      System.out.print("Enter Total No of unknown in the objective fn including slack /surplus ");

     n = input.nextInt();

     System.out.print("No of slack variables <= in the Constrain Equations");

 int  m1 = input.nextInt();
System.out.print("No of surplus variables >= in the Constrain Equations");

 int  m2 = input.nextInt();
m=m1+m2;
 float c[]= new  float[10];
 float b[]= new  float[10]; 
 float a[][]= new  float[10][10];     

    System.out.println("Enter value of c, coefficient of X in Z=c1x1 + c2x2 ");

 for (int j=1; j<=n; j++)
      {
         c[j]=input.nextFloat();
       }

     System.out.println("Enter value of aij, coefficient of X in in each  constrain equn.& thn b[i] value ");

 for (int i=1; i<=m; i++)
     {
     System.out.println("No= "+i) ;
 for (int j=1; j<=n; j++)
        {

         a[i][j]=input.nextFloat();

        }
       b[i]=input.nextFloat();

     }       

SP_LinerProg lnrg = new SP_LinerProg (n,m,c,a,b) ;
            }

}

import java.io.*;

public  class SP_Genetic2
{

 double []fitness= new  double [500];
 int []gDecimal=new  int [20];

 double crossover_rate=0.7 ;
 double mutation_rate=0.001;
 public  float target;
 //  int chromLength=24;
 int population=400;
 //  int population=40 ;
 int chromLength=18;
 int geneLength=6;
 //  int geneLength=8;
 int Max_No_Generation=200;

 public  boolean solutionFound;
 // public float target;

 // constructor
 public SP_Genetic2( )
    {
 //  Generate chromosome population

      System.out.println("Enter Target value");
Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);

 double target = input.nextDouble();
 for(j=1;j<=population; j++)
  {                          
c[j] = "";
 int temp = j;
 for (i=1; i<=chromLength;i++)
            {
Random rn = new Random();

 int rnd = rn.nextInt(1000);

 double  rf=(double)rnd/1000;

 if (rf<=0.5)
c[j] = '1'+c[j];
 else
c[j] = '0'+c[j];
                              }
 //  System.out.println(c[j]);      
    } 

//    c[1]="01101"; c[2]="1100"; c[3]="0101"; c[4]="1110"; c[5]="0011";

 int No_of_genration=1;
solutionFound=false;

while (!solutionFound)// Loop will continue till solutionFound
{ // is not false i e true i e solution found

 // Initially make all population fitness=0

 for(j=1;j<=population; j++)
   {
      fitness[j] =0.0;
 // System.out.print(<" fitness= "+fitness[j]);

   }
System.out.println();
// find fitness from chromo value
 for(j=1;j<=population; j++)
   {

    chromo_Value( c[j]);
//System.out.print( " chromo_Value( c[j])= "+chromo_Value( c[j])) ;

chromo_fitness(chromoValue[j]);
  System.out.println();
System.out.println( " chromo_fitness(chromoValue[j]= "+chromo_fitness(chromoValue[j]));

//Test if any chromosome has achieved required fitness
 if(fitness[j]>=0.01 )
{
   System.out.println("Bset fit chromosome found="+ c[j]);
   System.out.println("No_of_population "+population);
  System.out.println("No of Generation="+No_of_genration);
   System.out.println("ChromeValue="+chromoValue[j]);
   System.out.println("fitness= "+fitness[j]);

    System.out.println("Gene combiations are");

 for(i=1;i<=(chromLength/geneLength);i++)
  {
    System.out.println(" No="+i+" Gene (Binary)"+g[i]+"="+gDecimal[i]);
   }
  System.out.println();

    solutionFound=true;
 break;

  }

   }

float totalfitness=0;
for(j=1;j<=population; j++)
   {
         totalfitness +=fitness[j];  
   }

String offspring1;
String offspring2;
int pop =1;
  j=1;
 do {
offspring1 = Rwheel(totalfitness);
offspring2 = Rwheel(totalfitness);

//crossover
crossover (offspring1,offspring2 );

// mutate

  mutate (offspring1);
  mutate (offspring2);
   c[pop]=offspring1;
  c[pop+1]=offspring2;

pop= pop+2;
}while(pop<=population);

No_of_genration=No_of_genration+1;

 if (No_of_genration> Max_No_Generation )
  {
System.out.println("solutions not found after"+(No_of_genration-1)+" No of Generations" );
   solutionFound=true;
  }
}
}       

// All functions

// assign fitness
public  double chromo_fitness(double value)
{

fitness[j]= 1/Math.abs((target-chromoValue[j]));
if (target==chromoValue[j])
fitness[j]=100.0;
return fitness[j];
}

public  double chromo_Value( String chromo)
{
 //split each chromosome population into genes
int geneNo=1;
 for (i=0; i<chromLength;i+=geneLength)
  {
 // System.out.println ("c[j]"+ c[j] );

       g[geneNo]=c[j].substring(i,geneLength+i);

//System.out.print ("  g"+geneNo+"="+ g[geneNo]);

  geneNo=geneNo+1;
  }
System.out.println();
// convert each gene from binary to decimal integer
 for(geneNo=1;geneNo<=(chromLength/geneLength); geneNo++)
   {
 int temp=0,sum=0;

 for(k=0;k<geneLength;k++)
        {

 if (g[geneNo].charAt(k)=='1')
   {
      temp=(int)Math.pow(2,(geneLength-(k+1)));

   }
 else
   {
      temp=0;
   }

   sum=sum +temp;
         }

   gDecimal[geneNo]=sum;
   System.out.print  ("  gDecimal"+geneNo+"="+gDecimal[geneNo]);

   }
System.out.println();

 // z=4x1 + 3x2 +6x3
 //constrains 2x1+3x2+2x3<=440,4x1+3x3<=470, 2x1+5x2<=430,

   chromoValue[j]=4*gDecimal[1]+ 3*gDecimal[2]+ 6*gDecimal[3];

 double c1=2*gDecimal[1]+ 3*gDecimal[2]+ 2*gDecimal[3];
 double c2=4*gDecimal[1]+ 3*gDecimal[3];
 double c3=2*gDecimal[1]+ 5*gDecimal[2];
//System.out.println ( "C1 C2 C3 =" +c1+" "+c2+" "+c3  );

 if( c1>440 || c2>470 || c3>430)
      chromoValue[j]=-500;
// System.out.println ( "chromoValue[j]=" +chromoValue[j]   );
 return chromoValue[j];
}

public  void mutate( String offspr)
{
 double randf= randf=Math.random();
// cout<<"randf"<<randf;
 if (randf<mutation_rate)
   {
 for (i=0; i<offspr.length(); i++)
   {
 char ch =offspr.charAt(i);
 if(offspr.charAt(i)=='1')
         ch='0';
 //   offspr.charAt(i) ='0'; 
 else
 //   offspr.charAt(i)='1';
          ch='1';
   }   

   }
}

public  void crossover (String offspring1, String  offspring2)
{
int cross_pt ;

float randf=randf=(float)Math.random();
//cout<<"randf"<<randf;

if(randf <crossover_rate)
{
cross_pt = (int)(randf*chromLength);

String s1= offspring1.substring(0,cross_pt) + offspring2.substring(cross_pt,chromLength);
String s2= offspring2.substring(0,cross_pt) + offspring1.substring(cross_pt,chromLength);
offspring1=s1;
offspring2= s2;
}
}

public String Rwheel( float Totalfitness)
{
double randf= randf= Math.random();

double slice = (randf*Totalfitness);

float fitnessSoFar=0;
 for(j=1;j<=population; j++)
   {

   fitnessSoFar +=fitness[j];

 if(fitnessSoFar>=slice)
   {

 return c[j];}

   }
 return  "";

}               
 public  static  void main(String args[])
       {
 int n,m  ;
Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);
SP_Genetic2 gn = new SP_Genetic2() ;

      }                         
  }

Program 6.4.1  SP_Mapminmax.java

   import java.util.Scanner;
 import java.io.*;
 import java.math.*;
 import java.util.*;

 class SP_Mapminmax{

 public  static  void main(String args[])

{
 double array[] = new  double[]{10, 11, 88, 2, 12, 120};
 double x[]= new  double[6];
 // Calling getMax() method for getting max value
 double max = getMax(array);
    System.out.println("Maximum Value is: "+max);

 // Calling getMin() method for getting min value
 double min = getMin(array);
    System.out.println("Minimum Value is: "+min);
 double y[]=getMinMax(array);
         System.out.println ();

        System.out.print ("{");

 for(int i=0;i <6;i++)
    {
       System.out.print(" "+y[i] +"," );
     }
     System.out.print ("}");

        reverseMinMax(y);

        }
 // Method for getting the maximum value
 public  static  double getMax(double[] array)
  {
 double maxValue = array[0];
 for(int i=1;i < array.length;i++)
    {
 if(array[i] > maxValue){
         maxValue = array[i];
      }
    }
 return maxValue;
  }

 public  static  double getMin(double[] array)
  {
 double minValue = array[0];
 for(int i=1;i<array.length;i++)
    {
 if(array[i] < minValue){
        minValue = array[i];
      }
    }
 return minValue;
  }
 // Method for minmax
 public  static  double [] getMinMax(double[] array)
  {
 double xmax = getMax(array);
 double xmin = getMin(array);
 double ymax=1; double ymin=-1 ;
 double y[]= new  double[6];
 for(int i=0;i < array.length;i++)
    {
       y[i] = (ymax-ymin)*(array[i]-xmin)/(xmax-xmin) + ymin;
    }

     System.out.print ("{");

 for(int i=0;i < array.length;i++)
    {
       System.out.print(" "+y[i] +"," );
     }
     System.out.print ("}");

 return y;
  }
 // Method for reverse minmax
 public  static  double [] reverseMinMax(double[] y)
  {
 double array[] = new  double[]{10, 11, 88, 2, 12, 120};
 double xmax = getMax(array);
 double xmin = getMin(array);

 double ymax=1; double ymin=-1 ;
 //  double  y[]= new double[6];
 double x[]= new  double[6];

 for(int i=0;i < y.length;i++)
    {
 // y[i] = (ymax-ymin)*(array[i]-xmin)/(xmax-xmin) + ymin;
       x[i]=  (xmax-xmin)*(y[i]-ymin)/(ymax-ymin)+xmin;
          }
    System.out.println ();

     System.out.print ("{");

 for(int i=0;i < array.length;i++)
    {
       System.out.print(" "+x[i] +"," );
     }
     System.out.print ("}");

 return x;
  }
}

Program 6.4.2  SRP_NNet2.java

Remember  to run this program you will need three  Other java programs stored in sp_package:

1.SP_Mapminmax_pack.java,    2. SP_Neuron.java,   3.SP_Connection.java

import sp_package.*;
import java.text.*;
import java.util.*;

//Other java files taken from sp_package:1.SP_Mapminmax_pack.java,  2.SP_Neuron.java,  3.SP_Connection.java 
// class SP_Mapminmax_pack needs to be loaded to convert input data dimensionless & result back to Non dimensionless

public  class SRP_NNet2
{
 static {
        Locale.setDefault(Locale.ENGLISH);
    }

 final  boolean isTrained = false;
 final DecimalFormat df;
 final Random rand = new Random();
 final ArrayList<SP_Neuron> inputLayer = new ArrayList<SP_Neuron>();
 final ArrayList<SP_Neuron> hiddenLayer = new ArrayList<SP_Neuron>();
 final ArrayList<SP_Neuron> outputLayer = new ArrayList<SP_Neuron>();
 final SP_Neuron bias = new SP_Neuron();
 final  int[] layers;
 final  int randomWeightMultiplier = 1;

 final  double epsilon = 0.00000000001;

 final  double learningRate = 0.9f;
 final  double momentum = 0.7f;

 double output[];

 // for weight update all
 final HashMap<String, Double> weightUpdate = new HashMap<String, Double>();

 public  static  void main(String[] args)
    {
Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);

         System.out.println (" Enter no of input layer, No of hidden layer and No of output");

 int m=input.nextInt();
 int hdn=input.nextInt();
 int nm =input.nextInt();

        System.out.println ("Enter No of set of data of Input & corresponding ouput");

 int n=input.nextInt(); // size of data set

 double arr[][] = new  double [n][m]; // input array
 double arro[][]= new  double [n][nm];// output array

 for (int i=0; i<n; i++)
    {
      System.out.println(" Enter the inputs data X1, X2, X3 when output Y=f(X1,X2,X3) " );
 for (int j=0; j<m; j++)
       {
       arr [i][j]=input.nextDouble();
       }
       System.out.println(" Enter the corresponding output Y" );

 for (int j=0; j<nm; j++)
       {
       arro [i][j]=input.nextDouble();
       }
     } 

 // call class to convert input data dimensionless & result back        
SP_Mapminmax_pack  mp = new SP_Mapminmax_pack() ;

 double ixmax[]=new  double [m];// max array  of input data of each variable
 double ixmin[]=new  double[m]; // min arrayof input data of each variable

 double oxmax[]=new  double [nm]; // max array  of output data of each variable
 double oxmin[]=new  double  [nm];//// min array  of output data of each variable

 double xx[]= new  double[n];

 for ( int j=0;j<m; j++)
         {
 for(int i=0; i<n; i++)
           {
            xx[i]=arr[i][j];
            }
            ixmax[j] =mp.getMax(xx);
            ixmin[j] = mp.getMin(xx);
 // call getMax fn from class to find max of input data array of a single variable
 // similarly call getMin fn

            System.out.println ("input min max="+ixmin[j]+" "+ixmax[j]);

           }

 for ( int j=0;j<nm; j++)
         {
 for(int i=0; i<n; i++)
           {
            xx[i]=arro[i][j];

           }
            oxmax[j] =mp.getMax(xx);// same thing for output data
            oxmin[j] = mp.getMin(xx);

            System.out.println ("output min max="+oxmin[j]+" "+oxmax[j]);

           }

 doubleMMX [][] =mp.dataMMX (n,m,arr ); // get dimensionless input data array

 double inputs [][]= new  double [n][m];

 for (int i=0;i<n; i++)
      {
 for(int j=0;j<m; j++)
         {
          inputs[i][j]= MMX[i][j];
          }
       }
 // for output  
 doubleMMX2 [][] =mp.dataMMX (n,nm,arro ); // get dimensionless output data array

 double expectedOutputs[][] = new  double [n][nm];

 for (int i=0;i<n ; i++)
      {
 for(int j=0;j<nm; j++)
         {
             expectedOutputs[i][j]= MMX2[i][j];
         }
       }

 // call NNet constructor feeding  relevant data, input, hidden, ouputlayer,
 // no of set of set data n, all inputs & outputs in dimensionless form
SRP_NNet2 nn = new SRP_NNet2(m,hdn,nm,n,inputs,expectedOutputs);
 int maxRuns = 50000;
 double minErrorCondition = 0.001;
        nn.run(m,hdn,nm,n,inputs,expectedOutputs,ixmin,ixmax,oxmin,oxmax,maxRuns, minErrorCondition);
         }// End of main

 // start of constructor
 public SRP_NNet2(int input, int hidden, int output,int n,double [][] inputs,double [][] expectedOutputs)
     {
 this.layers = new  int[] { input, hidden, output };
df = new DecimalFormat("#.0#");
 // System.out.println ("input, hidden, output"+input +hidden+output);

 /**
         * Create all neurons and connections Connections are created in the
         * neuron class
         */
 int nm= output;
 int m=input;
 // System.out.println ("layers.length="+layers.length  );

       layers[0]=input; layers[1]=hidden; layers[2]= output;

 for (int i = 0; i < layers.length; i++)
        {
 if (i == 0)
{ // input layer
 for (int j = 0; j < layers[i]; j++)
                 {
SP_Neuron neuron = new SP_Neuron();
                    inputLayer.add(neuron);
                }
              }
 else  if (i == 1)
{ // hidden layer
 for (int j = 0; j < layers[i]; j++)
                {
SP_Neuron neuron = new SP_Neuron();
                    neuron.addInConnectionsS(inputLayer);
                    neuron.addBiasConnection(bias);
                    hiddenLayer.add(neuron);
                }
              }

 else  if (i == 2)
{ // output layer
 for (int j = 0; j < layers[i]; j++)
                  {
SP_Neuron neuron = new SP_Neuron();
                  neuron.addInConnectionsS(hiddenLayer);
                    neuron.addBiasConnection(bias);
                    outputLayer.add(neuron);
                  }
              }
 else {
                System.out.println("!Error NeuralNetwork init");
                  }
        }

 // initialize random weights
 for (SP_Neuron neuron : hiddenLayer)
        {
            ArrayList<SP_Connection> connections = neuron.getAllInConnections();
 for (SP_Connection conn : connections)
             {
 double newWeight = getRandom();
                conn.setWeight(newWeight);
              }
        }

 for (SP_Neuron neuron : outputLayer)
        {
            ArrayList<SP_Connection> connections = neuron.getAllInConnections();
 for (SP_Connection conn : connections)
            {
 double newWeight = getRandom();
                conn.setWeight(newWeight);
            }
        }

 // reset id counters
        SP_Neuron.counter = 0;
        SP_Connection.counter = 0;

 if (isTrained) {
            trainedWeights();
            updateAllWeights();
        }
    }

 // random
 double getRandom()
    {
 return randomWeightMultiplier * (rand.nextDouble() * 2 - 1); // [-1;1[
    }

 /**
     *
     * @param inputs
     *            There is equally many neurons in the input layer as there are
     *            in input variables
     */
 public  void setInput(double inputs[])
    {
 // System.out.println ("inputLayer.size()="+inputLayer.size());

 for (int i = 0; i <inputLayer.size(); i++)
        {
            inputLayer.get(i).setOutput(inputs[i]);
        }
     }

 public  double[] getOutput()
     {
 double[] outputs = new  double[outputLayer.size()];
 // System.out.println ("outputLayer.size()="+outputLayer.size());

 for (int i = 0; i <outputLayer.size(); i++)
            outputs[i] = outputLayer.get(i).getOutput();
 return outputs;
    }

 /**
     * Calculate the output of the neural network based on the input The forward
     * operation
     */
 public  void activate()
     {
 for (SP_Neuron n : hiddenLayer)
            n.calculateOutput();
 for (SP_Neuron n : outputLayer)
            n.calculateOutput();
     }

 /**
     * all output propagate back
     *
     * @param expectedOutput
     *  first calculate the partial derivative of the error with
     *  respect to each of the weight leading into the output neurons
     *   bias is also updated here
     */
 public  void applyBackpropagation(double expectedOutput[])
    {

 // error check, normalize value ]0;1[
 //   System.out.println ("expectedOutput.length="+expectedOutput.length);
 for (int i = 0; i <expectedOutput.length; i++)
        {
 double d = expectedOutput[i];
 if (d < 0 || d > 1)
            {
 if (d < 0)
                    expectedOutput[i] = 0 + epsilon;
 else
                    expectedOutput[i] = 1 - epsilon;
            }
         }

 int i = 0;
 for (SP_Neuron n : outputLayer)
         {
            ArrayList<SP_Connection> connections = n.getAllInConnections();
 for (SP_Connection con : connections)
            {
 double ak = n.getOutput();
 double ai = con.leftNeuron.getOutput();
 double desiredOutput = expectedOutput[i];

 double partialDerivative = -ak * (1 - ak) * ai
                        * (desiredOutput - ak);
 double deltaWeight = -learningRate * partialDerivative;
 double newWeight = con.getWeight() + deltaWeight;
                con.setDeltaWeight(deltaWeight);
                con.setWeight(newWeight + momentum * con.getPrevDeltaWeight());
            }
            i++;
        }

 // update weights for the hidden layer
 for (SP_Neuron n : hiddenLayer) {
            ArrayList<SP_Connection> connections = n.getAllInConnections();
 for (SP_Connection con : connections) {
 double aj = n.getOutput();
 double ai = con.leftNeuron.getOutput();
 double sumKoutputs = 0;
 int j = 0;
 for (SP_Neuron out_neu : outputLayer) {
 double wjk = out_neu.getConnection(n.id).getWeight();
 double desiredOutput = (double) expectedOutput[j];
 double ak = out_neu.getOutput();
                    j++;
                    sumKoutputs = sumKoutputs
                            + (-(desiredOutput - ak) * ak * (1 - ak) * wjk);
                }

 double partialDerivative = aj * (1 - aj) * ai * sumKoutputs;
 double deltaWeight = -learningRate * partialDerivative;
 double newWeight = con.getWeight() + deltaWeight;
                con.setDeltaWeight(deltaWeight);
                con.setWeight(newWeight + momentum * con.getPrevDeltaWeight());
            }
        }
    }
// Run NN with all feeding data plus min & max of original input output
// to convert back the result to non dimension form i e real number
 void run(int m,int hdn,int nm,int n,double [][]inputs, double [][] expectedOutputs,double[]ixmin,double[]ixmax,double[]oxmin, double[]oxmax, int maxSteps, double minError)
    {
 int i;
 double resultOutputs[ ][]= new double[n][nm];
 for (i=0; i<n; i++)
{ for (int j=0; j<nm; j++)
        { resultOutputs[i][j]=-1;}}

 // Train neural network until minError reached or maxSteps exceeded
 double error = 1;
 for (i = 0; i < maxSteps && error > minError; i++)
         {
 //  System.out.println("inputs.length= "+inputs.length);
            error = 0;
 for (int p = 0; p <n; p++)
            {
                setInput(inputs[p]);

                activate();

                output = getOutput();
                resultOutputs[p] = output;

 for (int j = 0; j < nm; j++)
                 {

 double err = Math.pow((output[j] - expectedOutputs[p][j]), 2);
                    error += err;
                }

                applyBackpropagation(expectedOutputs[p]);
            }
        }

 double arr[][] = new  double [n][m]; // Read input
 double arro[][]= new  double [n][nm];// Read actual output

 for (int p = 0; p < n; p++)
        {
            System.out.print("INPUTS: ");

            layers[0]=m; layers[2]=nm;

 for (int x = 0; x < layers[0]; x++)
            {
System.out.print(inputs[p][x] + " ");
                arr[p][x]=inputs[p][x];
            }

            System.out.print("EXPECTED: ");
 for (int x = 0; x < layers[2]; x++)
             {
System.out.print(expectedOutputs[p][x] + " ");
            }

            System.out.print("ACTUAL: ");
 for (int x = 0; x < layers[2]; x++)
            {
System.out.print(resultOutputs[p][x] + " ");
                  arro[p][x]=resultOutputs[p][x];
            }
            System.out.println();
        }
 // call the class to convert back to number , the dimensionless result

SRP_Mapminmax_pack  mp = new SRP_Mapminmax_pack() ;
 // max & min of original non dimenisionless data  to be given
 // i-input o-output               

 double  MMR [][] =mp.dataMMR  ( n, m,arr,ixmin,ixmax );

 double  MMR2 [][] =mp.dataMMR  ( n, nm,arro,oxmin,oxmax );

       System.out.println("Input and output Results after NN");
 for (int p = 0; p < n; p++)
       {
 for (int x = 0; x < layers[0]; x++)
         {
           System.out.print(" "+MMR [p][x]);
          }

 for (int x = 0; x < layers[2]; x++)
          {
           System.out.println(" "+MMR2 [p][x]);
          }
                   }

        System.out.println("Sum of squared errors = " + error);
        System.out.println("##### EPOCH " + i+"\n");
 if (i == maxSteps) {
            System.out.println("!Error training try again");
} else {
            printAllWeights();
            printWeightUpdate();
        }
    }    

    String weightKey(int neuronId, int conId)
    {
 return  "N" + neuronId + "_C" + conId;
    }

 /**
     * Take from hash table and put into all weights
     */
 public  void updateAllWeights()
    {
 // update weights for the output layer
 for (SP_Neuron n : outputLayer)
          {
            ArrayList<SP_Connection> connections = n.getAllInConnections();
 for (SP_Connection con : connections) {
                String key = weightKey(n.id, con.id);
 double newWeight = weightUpdate.get(key);
                con.setWeight(newWeight);
            }
        }
 // update weights for the hidden layer
 for (SP_Neuron n : hiddenLayer)
         {
            ArrayList<SP_Connection> connections = n.getAllInConnections();
 for (SP_Connection con : connections) {
                String key = weightKey(n.id, con.id);
 double newWeight = weightUpdate.get(key);
                con.setWeight(newWeight);
            }
        }
    }

 // trained data
 void trainedWeights()
    {
        weightUpdate.clear();

        weightUpdate.put(weightKey(3, 0), 1.03);
        weightUpdate.put(weightKey(3, 1), 1.13);
        weightUpdate.put(weightKey(3, 2), -.97);
        weightUpdate.put(weightKey(4, 3), 7.24);
        weightUpdate.put(weightKey(4, 4), -3.71);
        weightUpdate.put(weightKey(4, 5), -.51);
        weightUpdate.put(weightKey(5, 6), -3.28);
        weightUpdate.put(weightKey(5, 7), 7.29);
        weightUpdate.put(weightKey(5, 8), -.05);
        weightUpdate.put(weightKey(6, 9), 5.86);
        weightUpdate.put(weightKey(6, 10), 6.03);
        weightUpdate.put(weightKey(6, 11), .71);
        weightUpdate.put(weightKey(7, 12), 2.19);
        weightUpdate.put(weightKey(7, 13), -8.82);
        weightUpdate.put(weightKey(7, 14), -8.84);
        weightUpdate.put(weightKey(7, 15), 11.81);
        weightUpdate.put(weightKey(7, 16), .44);
    }

 public  void printWeightUpdate() {
 // weights for the hidden layer
 for (SP_Neuron n : hiddenLayer) {
            ArrayList<SP_Connection> connections = n.getAllInConnections();
 for (SP_Connection con : connections) {
                String w = df.format(con.getWeight());
            }
        }
 // weights for the output layer
 for (SP_Neuron n : outputLayer) {
            ArrayList<SP_Connection> connections = n.getAllInConnections();
 for (SP_Connection con : connections) {
                String w = df.format(con.getWeight());
           }
        }
 // System.out.println();
    }

 public  void printAllWeights() {
 //    System.out.println("printAllWeights");
 // weights for the hidden layer
 for (SP_Neuron n : hiddenLayer) {
            ArrayList<SP_Connection> connections = n.getAllInConnections();
 for (SP_Connection con : connections) {
 double w = con.getWeight();
         }
        }
 // weights for the output layer
 for (SP_Neuron n : outputLayer) {
            ArrayList<SP_Connection> connections = n.getAllInConnections();
 for (SP_Connection con : connections) {
 double w = con.getWeight();
                          }
        }
     }
}

Program 6.5.1 SRP_NNData.java 

    import java.util.Scanner;
 import java.io.DataOutputStream;

 public  class SRP_NNData
    {

 public  static  void main(String[] args)
        {
Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);

         System.out.println (" Enter no of input layer,   and No of output");
 int m=input.nextInt();
 // int hdn=input.nextInt();
 int nm =input.nextInt();

        System.out.println ("Enter No of set of data of Input & corresponding ouput");

 int n=input.nextInt(); // size of data set

 double arr[][] = new  double [n][m]; // input array
 double arro[][]= new  double [n][nm];// output array

 for (int i=0; i<n; i++)
    {
      System.out.println(" Enter the inputs data X1, X2, X3 when output Y=f(X1,X2,X3) " );
 for (int j=0; j<m; j++)
       {
       arr [i][j]=input.nextDouble();
       }
       System.out.println(" Enter the corresponding output Y" );

 for (int j=0; j<nm; j++)
       {
       arro [i][j]=input.nextDouble();
       }
     }

String strFilePath = "E://Java_Engg_Problem/dataNN.txt";

 try{           
             FileOutputStream file=new FileOutputStream(strFilePath);
DataOutputStream fout = new DataOutputStream(file);

             fout.writeInt(m);
 //  fout.writeInt(hdn);
             fout.writeInt(nm);
             fout.writeInt(n);

 for (int i=0; i<n; i++)
               {
 for (int j=0; j<m; j++)
                  {

                   fout.writeDouble(arr [i][j]);
                   }

 for (int j=0; j<nm; j++)
                  {
                  fout.writeDouble(arro [i][j]);
                  }
              }

         fout.close();                
        System.out.println("success...");                  
       }

 catch(Exception e)
          {
                System.out.println(e);
           }   

         }                
    }

Program 6.5.2  SRP_NNetFileData2,java

import sp_package.*;
import java.text.*;
import java.util.*;
import java.io.DataInputStream;
import java.io.FileInputStream;
import java.io.FileNotFoundException;
import java.io.IOException;

//Other java files taken from sp_package:1.SP_Mapminmax_pack.java,2\. SP_Neuron.java, 3.SP_Connection.java 
// class SP_Mapminmax_pack needs to be loaded to convert input data dimensionless & result back to Non dimensionless

public  class SRP_NNetFileData2 {
 static {
        Locale.setDefault(Locale.ENGLISH);
    }

 final  boolean isTrained = false;
 final DecimalFormat df;
 final Random rand = new Random();
 final ArrayList<SP_Neuron> inputLayer = new ArrayList<SP_Neuron>();
 final ArrayList<SP_Neuron> hiddenLayer = new ArrayList<SP_Neuron>();
 final ArrayList<SP_Neuron> outputLayer = new ArrayList<SP_Neuron>();
 final SP_Neuron bias = new SP_Neuron();
 final  int[] layers;
 final  int randomWeightMultiplier = 1;

 final  double epsilon = 0.00000000001;

 final  double learningRate = 0.9f;
 final  double momentum = 0.7f;

 double output[];

 // for weight update all
 final HashMap<String, Double> weightUpdate = new HashMap<String, Double>();

 public  static  void main(String[] args)
    {
 int m,nm,n,hdn;
        n=1;m=1;nm=1;hdn=1;
 double arr[][] = new  double [40][15]; // input array
 double arro[][]= new  double [40][5];// output array

Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);

         System.out.println (" Do you want to read NN data from a file if yes  press 1 else 2");
 int y=input.nextInt();
 if (y==1)
         {
System.out.println ( "Reading Data from File");

String strFilePath = "E://Java_Engg_Problem/dataNN.txt";  

 try
         {
FileInputStream fin = new FileInputStream(strFilePath);

DataInputStream din = new DataInputStream(fin);

           m=din.readInt();
 //   hdn=din.readInt();
          nm =din.readInt();
           n=din.readInt();

 for (int i=0; i<n; i++)
         {
 for (int j=0; j<m; j++)
             {
               arr [i][j]=din.readDouble() ;
              }

 for (int j=0; j<nm; j++)
            {
               arro [i][j]=din.readDouble();
             }
          } 

      din.close();

      }
 catch(FileNotFoundException fe)
       {
      System.out.println("FileNotFoundException : " + fe);
      }
 catch(IOException ioe)
      {
      System.out.println("IOException : " + ioe);
      } 
System.out.println ( "Choose No of hidden layer ,select (1 to 2) less or more than no of Input layer,if input layer=3 hidden layer may be 2 or 4 etc");
       hdn=input.nextInt();     
    }

 else
      {  

        System.out.println (" Enter no of input layer, No of hidden layer and No of output");
          m=input.nextInt();
         hdn=input.nextInt();
          nm =input.nextInt();

        System.out.println ("Enter No of set of data of Input & corresponding ouput");

n=input.nextInt(); // size of data set                       

 for (int i=0; i<n; i++)
    {
      System.out.println(" Enter the inputs data X1, X2, X3 when output Y=f(X1,X2,X3) " );
 for (int j=0; j<m; j++)

 // start of constructor
 public SRP_NNetFileData2(int input, int hidden, int output,int n,double [][] inputs,double [][] expectedOutputs)
     {
 this.layers = new  int[] { input, hidden, output };
df = new DecimalFormat("#.0#");
 // System.out.println ("input, hidden, output"+input +hidden+output);

 /**
         * Create all neurons and connections Connections are created in the
         * neuron class
         */
 int nm= output;
 int m=input;
 // System.out.println ("layers.length="+layers.length  );

       layers[0]=input; layers[1]=hidden; layers[2]= output;

 for (int i = 0; i < layers.length; i++)
        {
 if (i == 0)
{ // input layer
 for (int j = 0; j < layers[i]; j++)
                 {
SP_Neuron neuron = new SP_Neuron();
                    inputLayer.add(neuron);
                }
              }
 else  if (i == 1)
{ // hidden layer
 for (int j = 0; j < layers[i]; j++)
                {
SP_Neuron neuron = new SP_Neuron();
                    neuron.addInConnectionsS(inputLayer);
                    neuron.addBiasConnection(bias);
                    hiddenLayer.add(neuron);
                }
              }

 else  if (i == 2)
{ // output layer
 for (int j = 0; j < layers[i]; j++)
                  {
SP_Neuron neuron = new SP_Neuron();
                  neuron.addInConnectionsS(hiddenLayer);
                    neuron.addBiasConnection(bias);
                    outputLayer.add(neuron);
                  }
              }
 else {
                System.out.println("!Error NeuralNetwork init");
                  }
        }

 // initialize random weights
 for (SP_Neuron neuron : hiddenLayer)
        {
            ArrayList<SP_Connection> connections = neuron.getAllInConnections();
 for (SP_Connection conn : connections)
             {
 double newWeight = getRandom();
                conn.setWeight(newWeight);
              }
        }

 for (SP_Neuron neuron : outputLayer)
        {
            ArrayList<SP_Connection> connections = neuron.getAllInConnections();
 for (SP_Connection conn : connections)
            {
 double newWeight = getRandom();
                conn.setWeight(newWeight);
            }
        }

 // reset id counters
        SP_Neuron.counter = 0;
        SP_Connection.counter = 0;

 if (isTrained) {
            trainedWeights();
            updateAllWeights();
        }
    }

 // random
 double getRandom()
    {
 return randomWeightMultiplier * (rand.nextDouble() * 2 - 1); // [-1;1[
    }

 /**
     *
     * @param inputs
     *            There is equally many neurons in the input layer as there are
     *            in input variables
     */
 public  void setInput(double inputs[])
    {
 // System.out.println ("inputLayer.size()="+inputLayer.size());

 for (int i = 0; i <inputLayer.size(); i++)
        {
            inputLayer.get(i).setOutput(inputs[i]);
        }
     }

 public  double[] getOutput()
     {
 double[] outputs = new  double[outputLayer.size()];
 // System.out.println ("outputLayer.size()="+outputLayer.size());

 for (int i = 0; i <outputLayer.size(); i++)
            outputs[i] = outputLayer.get(i).getOutput();
 return outputs;
    }

 /**
     * Calculate the output of the neural network based on the input The forward
     * operation
     */
 public  void activate()
     {
 for (SP_Neuron n : hiddenLayer)
            n.calculateOutput();
 for (SP_Neuron n : outputLayer)
            n.calculateOutput();
     }

 /**
     * all output propagate back
     *
     * @param expectedOutput
     *  first calculate the partial derivative of the error with
     *  respect to each of the weight leading into the output neurons
     *   bias is also updated here
     */
 public  void applyBackpropagation(double expectedOutput[])
    {

 // error check, normalize value ]0;1[
 //   System.out.println ("expectedOutput.length="+expectedOutput.length);
 for (int i = 0; i <expectedOutput.length; i++)
        {
 double d = expectedOutput[i];
 if (d < 0 || d > 1)
            {
 if (d < 0)
                    expectedOutput[i] = 0 + epsilon;
 else
                    expectedOutput[i] = 1 - epsilon;
            }
         }

 int i = 0;
 for (SP_Neuron n : outputLayer)
         {
            ArrayList<SP_Connection> connections = n.getAllInConnections();
 for (SP_Connection con : connections)
            {
 double ak = n.getOutput();
 double ai = con.leftNeuron.getOutput();
 double desiredOutput = expectedOutput[i];

 double partialDerivative = -ak * (1 - ak) * ai
                        * (desiredOutput - ak);
 double deltaWeight = -learningRate * partialDerivative;
 double newWeight = con.getWeight() + deltaWeight;
                con.setDeltaWeight(deltaWeight);
                con.setWeight(newWeight + momentum * con.getPrevDeltaWeight());
            }
            i++;
        }

 // update weights for the hidden layer
 for (SP_Neuron n : hiddenLayer) {
            ArrayList<SP_Connection> connections = n.getAllInConnections();
 for (SP_Connection con : connections) {
 double aj = n.getOutput();
 double ai = con.leftNeuron.getOutput();
 double sumKoutputs = 0;
 int j = 0;
 for (SP_Neuron out_neu : outputLayer) {
 double wjk = out_neu.getConnection(n.id).getWeight();
 double desiredOutput = (double) expectedOutput[j];
 double ak = out_neu.getOutput();
                    j++;
                    sumKoutputs = sumKoutputs
                            + (-(desiredOutput - ak) * ak * (1 - ak) * wjk);
                }

 double partialDerivative = aj * (1 - aj) * ai * sumKoutputs;
 double deltaWeight = -learningRate * partialDerivative;
 double newWeight = con.getWeight() + deltaWeight;
                con.setDeltaWeight(deltaWeight);
                con.setWeight(newWeight + momentum * con.getPrevDeltaWeight());
            }
        }
    }
// Run NN with all feeding data plus min & max of original input output
// to convert back the result to non dimension form i e real number
 void run(int m,int hdn,int nm,int n,double [][]inputs, double [][] expectedOutputs,double[]ixmin,double[]ixmax,double[]oxmin, double[]oxmax, int maxSteps, double minError)
    {
 int i;
 double resultOutputs[ ][]= new double[n][nm];
 for (i=0; i<n; i++)
{ for (int j=0; j<nm; j++)
        { resultOutputs[i][j]=-1;}}

 // Train neural network until minError reached or maxSteps exceeded
 double error = 1;
 for (i = 0; i < maxSteps && error > minError; i++)
         {
 //  System.out.println("inputs.length= "+inputs.length);
            error = 0;
 for (int p = 0; p <n; p++)
            {
                setInput(inputs[p]);

                activate();

                output = getOutput();
                resultOutputs[p] = output;

 //   System.out.println("expectedOutputs[p].length= "+expectedOutputs[p].length);

 for (int j = 0; j < nm; j++)
                 {
 // System.out.println( "out, expec "+output[j]+expectedOutputs[p][j]);

 double err = Math.pow((output[j] - expectedOutputs[p][j]), 2);
                    error += err;
                }

                applyBackpropagation(expectedOutputs[p]);
            }
        }

 // printResult();
 // System.out.println (" printResult()inputs.length= "+inputs.length);
 double arr[][] = new  double [n][m]; // Read input
 double arro[][]= new  double [n][nm];// Read actual output

 for (int p = 0; p < n; p++)
        {
            System.out.print("INPUTS: ");

            layers[0]=m; layers[2]=nm;

 for (int x = 0; x < layers[0]; x++)
            {
System.out.print(inputs[p][x] + " ");
                arr[p][x]=inputs[p][x];
            }

            System.out.print("EXPECTED: ");
 for (int x = 0; x < layers[2]; x++)
             {
System.out.print(expectedOutputs[p][x] + " ");
            }

            System.out.print("ACTUAL: ");
 for (int x = 0; x < layers[2]; x++)
            {
System.out.print(resultOutputs[p][x] + " ");
                  arro[p][x]=resultOutputs[p][x];
            }
            System.out.println();
        }
 // call the class to convert back to number , the dimensionless result

SRP_Mapminmax_pack  mp = new SRP_Mapminmax_pack() ;
 // max & min of original non dimenisionless data  to be given
 // i-input o-output               

 double  MMR [][] =mp.dataMMR  ( n, m,arr,ixmin,ixmax );

 double  MMR2 [][] =mp.dataMMR  ( n, nm,arro,oxmin,oxmax );

       System.out.println("Input and output Results after NN");
 for (int p = 0; p < n; p++)
       {
 for (int x = 0; x < layers[0]; x++)
         {
           System.out.print(" "+MMR [p][x]);
          }

 for (int x = 0; x < layers[2]; x++)
          {
           System.out.println(" "+MMR2 [p][x]);
          }

         }

        System.out.println("Sum of squared errors = " + error);
        System.out.println("##### EPOCH " + i+"\n");
 if (i == maxSteps) {
            System.out.println("!Error training try again");
} else {
            printAllWeights();
            printWeightUpdate();
        }
    }    

    String weightKey(int neuronId, int conId)
    {
 return  "N" + neuronId + "_C" + conId;
    }

 /**
     * Take from hash table and put into all weights
     */
 public  void updateAllWeights()
    {
 // update weights for the output layer
 for (SP_Neuron n : outputLayer)
          {
            ArrayList<SP_Connection> connections = n.getAllInConnections();
 for (SP_Connection con : connections) {
                String key = weightKey(n.id, con.id);
 double newWeight = weightUpdate.get(key);

 for (SP_Connection con : connections) {

 double newWeight = weightUpdate.get(key);
                con.setWeight(newWeight);
            }
        }
    }

12.7 Chapter 7 Finite Element Method (FEM)

Program 7.5.1 SRP_FEM.java

import  static java.lang.Math.*;
import java.util.Scanner;
import java.io.DataOutputStream;
import java.io.FileOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
