import java.util.Scanner;
import java.math.*;
import java.util.Random;

import java.rmi.RemoteException;
import java.awt.*;
import javax.swing.*;
import java.awt.event.*;

public  class SRP_FEM
{
public triangular_element te[];
public setElement se[];
public  int N,M,K,K1,K2,Nm1,Mm1;
public  double x[][];
public  double y[][];
public  double T[][];
public  double Ti[];
public  double xi[];
public  double yi[];
public  boolean bi[];
public  double dx,dy;
public  int Bx0[],BxM[],By0[],ByN[];
public  double L,W;

public  double min(double xi[][])
{ //double min=0.002;
double min=1e200;
for(int i=0;i<xi.length;i++)
{for(int j=0;j<xi[0].length;j++)
{if(xi[i][j]<min) min=xi[i][j];}
}
return min;
}
public  double max(double xi[][])
{double max=-1e200;
//double max=-0.002;
for(int i=0;i<xi.length;i++)
{for(int j=0;j<xi[0].length;j++)
{if(xi[i][j]<max) max=xi[i][j];}
}
return max;
}
public SRP_FEM(double xi[][],double yi[][])
{x=xi;
y=yi;
N=x.length-1;
M=x[0].length-1;
T=new  double[N+1][M+1];
L=max(xi);
W=max(yi);
setElements();
}
public  void generateTriangularGrid(double Li, double Wi,int Ni, int Mi)
{int i,j;
N=Ni;M=Mi;
L=Li;
W=Wi;
x=new  double[N+1][M+1];
y=new  double[N+1][M+1];
T=new  double[N+1][M+1];
setElements();
}
public  void setElements()
{ int i,j;
dx=L/M;
dy=W/N;
K=M*N;
K1=K*2;
K2=K1+1;
//grid points
xi=new  double[K2];
yi=new  double[K2];
Ti=new  double[K2];
bi=new  boolean[K2];
se=new setElement[K2];
for(i=0;i<K2;i++) {se[i]=new setElement();}
te=new triangular_element[K1];
int k1=-1,k=0,n=0,m=0;
for(n=0;n<=N;n++)
{ for(m=0;m<=M;m++)
{k=(N+1)*n+m;
x[n][m]=m*dx;y[n][m]=n*dy;
xi[k]=x[n][m];
yi[k]=y[n][m];
bi[k]=false;
Ti[k]=0.0;
}
}
for(n=0;n<N;n++)
{for(m=0;m<M;m++)
{ k=(N+1)*n+m;
int kp1=(N+1)*(n+1)+m;
int kp1p1= kp1+1;
double xi1[]={x[n][m],x[n][m+1],x[n+1][m]};
double yi1[]={y[n][m],y[n][m+1],y[n+1][m]};
k1++;
int n_point1[]={k,(k+1),kp1};
se[k].add(k1,0);
se[k+1].add(k1,1);
se[kp1].add(k1,2);
te[k1]=new triangular_element(k1,n_point1,xi1,yi1);
double xi2[]={x[n][m+1],x[n+1][m+1],x[n+1][m]};
double yi2[]={y[n][m+1],y[n+1][m+1],y[n+1][m]};
k1++;
int n_point2[]={k+1,kp1p1,kp1};
se[k+1].add(k1,0);
se[kp1p1].add(k1,1);
se[kp1].add(k1,2);
te[k1]=new triangular_element(k1,n_point2,xi2,yi2);
}
}
}
public SRP_FEM(double Li, double Wi,int Ni, int Mi)
{

generateTriangularGrid(Li,Wi,Ni,Mi);

}
public  int find_element(double z[])
{
return find_element(z[0],z[1]);
}

public  int find_element(double x,double y)
{
double R1=Math.sqrt(x*x+y*y);
int kmin=0;
double min=1.0e50;
for(int k=0;k<K1;k++)
{
te[k].setN(x,y);
double r0=Math.sqrt((te[k].x[0]-x)*(te[k].x[0]-x)+(te[k].y[0]-y)*(te[k].y[0]-y));
double r1=Math.sqrt((te[k].x[1]-x)*(te[k].x[1]-x)+(te[k].y[1]-y)*(te[k].y[1]-y));
double r2=Math.sqrt((te[k].x[2]-x)*(te[k].x[2]-x)+(te[k].y[2]-y)*(te[k].y[2]-y));
double dr=Math.sqrt(r0*r0+r1*r1+r2*r2);
boolean b1=(x>=te[k].x[0]) && (x<=te[k].x[2]);
boolean b2=(y>=te[k].y[0]) && (y<=te[k].y[1]);
boolean b= b1 && b2;
if(dr<min) {min=dr;kmin=k;}
}
return kmin;
}

public  void setConvection(int cond,double L0,double L,double W0,double W, double hi, double tinfi)
{//cond 0: x==L0
//cond 1: x==L
//cond 2: y==W0
//cond 3: y==W
int j=0;
for(int i=0;i<K1;i++)
{ j=te[i].setI(cond,L0,L,W0,W);
te[i].setConvection(j,hi,tinfi);
}
}

public  void setHeatFlux(int cond,double L0,double L,double W0,double W, double qi)
{//cond 0: x==L0
//cond 1: x==L
//cond 2: y==W0
//cond 3: y==W
int j=-1;
for(int i=0;i<K1;i++)
{ j=te[i].setI(cond,L0,L,W0,W);
te[i].setHeatFlux(j,qi);
}
}

public  void setTemperature(int cond,double L0,double L,double W0,double W, double TTi)
{//cond 0: x==L0
//cond 1: x==L
//cond 2: y==W0
//cond 3: y==W
for(int i=0;i<K2;i++)
{ if(cond==0)
{if(xi[i]==L0) {Ti[i]=TTi;bi[i]=true;}}
else  if(cond==1)
{if(xi[i]==L) {Ti[i]=TTi;bi[i]=true;}}
else  if(cond==2)
{if(yi[i]==W0) {Ti[i]=TTi;bi[i]=true;}}
else  if(cond==3)
{if(yi[i]==W) {Ti[i]=TTi;bi[i]=true;}}
}
}

public  void setHeatGeneration(int node[],double qgeni)
{
int n=node.length;
for(int i=0;i<n;i++)
{
te[node[i]].setHeatGeneration(qgeni);
}
}

public  void solve(double ti,double k[])
{
double K[][]=new  double[K2][K2];
double KK[][]=new  double[K2][K2];
double ff[]=new  double[K2];
double KN[][]=new  double[3][3];
double f[]=new  double[K2];
double fN[]=new  double[3];

for(int i=0;i<K1;i++)
{KN=te[i].K(ti,k);
fN=te[i].f(ti,k);
int n1=te[i].n_element;
int m[]=te[i].n_point;
for(int j=0;j<3;j++)
{ for(int k1=0;k1<3;k1++)
{K[m[j]][m[k1]]+=KN[j][k1];KK[m[j]][m[k1]]=K[m[j]][m[k1]];
}
f[m[j]]+=fN[j];
ff[m[j]]=f[m[j]];
}
}
//redesign K matrix and f vector if any known
//temperature boundary is given
//int j=0;
for(int j=0;j<K2;j++)
{
for(int i=0;i<K2;i++)
{ if(bi[i])
{
if(i==j)
{f[i]=Ti[i];K[i][j]=1.0;}
else
{ K[i][j]=0.0;}

}
}//end of for
} //end of for
double TT[]=AXB(K,f);
int k1=0;
for(int n=0;n<=N;n++)
{ for(int m=0;m<=M;m++)
{k1=(N+1)*n+m;
T[n][m]=TT[k1];
}
}
}
//LU decomposition method
public  static  double[][] LU(double c[][],int indx[],int d[])
{
//returns LU decomposition of matrix c and index indx
double a[][];
int n=c.length;
a=new  double[n][n];
double vv[];
vv=new  double[n];
double sum,dum,big,temp;
int i,j,k;
int imax;
int nmax=100;
double tiny=1.0e-40;
imax=0;
for(i=1;i<=n;i++)
{
for(j=1;j<=n;j++)
a[i-1][j-1]=c[i-1][j-1];
}
d[0]=1;
for(i=1;i<=n;i++)
{
big=0.0;
for(j=1;j<=n;j++)
{
if(Math.abs(a[i-1][j-1])>big) big=Math.abs(a[i-1][j-1]);
}
if(big==0) {System.out.println("singular matrix");return a;}
vv[i-1]=1.0/big;
}
for(j=1;j<=n;j++)
{
for(i=1;i<j;i++)
{
sum=a[i-1][j-1];
for(k=1;k<i;k++)
{
sum-=a[i-1][k-1]*a[k-1][j-1];
}
a[i-1][j-1]=sum;
}
big=0;
for(i=j;i<=n;i++)
{
sum=a[i-1][j-1];
for(k=1;k<j;k++)
{
sum-=a[i-1][k-1]*a[k-1][j-1];
}
a[i-1][j-1]=sum;
dum=vv[i-1]*Math.abs(sum);
if(dum>=big)
{
imax=i;
big=dum;
}
} //end of i=0
if(j != imax)
{
for(k=1;k<=n;k++)
{
dum=a[imax-1][k-1];
a[imax-1][k-1]=a[j-1][k-1];
a[j-1][k-1]=dum;
}
d[0]=-d[0];
vv[imax-1]=vv[j-1];
} //end of if
indx[j-1]=imax;
if(a[j-1][j-1]==0) a[j-1][j-1]=tiny;
if(j!=n)
{
dum=1.0/a[j-1][j-1];
for(i=j+1;i<=n;i++)
a[i-1][j-1]*=dum;
}//endif
} //end for j=
return a;
}
public  static  double[] LUaxb(double a[][],double x[],int indx[])
{
//solves AX=B system of linear equation of LU decomposed matrix a
//(calculated by method LU)
int ii=0;
int i,j,ll=0;
double sum=0;
int n=a.length;
double b[];
b=new  double[n];
for(i=1;i<=n;i++)
{
b[i-1]=x[i-1];
}
for(i=1;i<=n;i++)
{
ll=indx[i-1];
sum=b[ll-1];
b[ll-1]=b[i-1];
if(ii!=0)
{
for(j=ii;j<=(i-1);j++)
{
sum-=a[i-1][j-1]*b[j-1];
}
}
else  if(sum!=0) ii=i;
b[i-1]=sum;
}
for(i=n;i>=1;i--)
{
sum=b[i-1];
if(i<n)
{
for(j=(i+1);j<=n;j++)
{
sum-=a[i-1][j-1]*b[j-1];
}
}
b[i-1]=sum/a[i-1][i-1];
}
return b;
}
public  static  double[] AXB(double a[][],double b[])
{
//Solution of system of linear equations by LU method
// note that the same calculation can be done by divide method.
int n=a.length;
double c[]=new  double[n];
int d[]={1};
int indx[]=new  int[n];
double e[][]=new  double[n][n];
e=LU(a,indx,d);
c=LUaxb(e,b,indx);
return c;
} //end of AXB

import java.io.*;

public  class SRP_FEM_UnsteadyState
{
 public  double  ap,r,lm,dt,dx,dtm,L,tm,t,cp,d,K,h,tc,sum,Tf,Ti,T0,Ts;  
 public  int i,j,n,nn,m,mm,jj,k,kk,l;
 double  xboundary1,xboundary2,x0;      
 double [] T= new  double [100]  ;
 int N= 40;
 double [][]a = new  double[N][N];
 double [][]al=new  double[N][N];
 double [][]au=new  double[N][N];
 double []x= new  double[N];
 double []b=new  double[N];
 double []z=new  double[N];
 double  [][]B=new  double[N][N];
 double[][] D=new  double[N][N];
 double [][]E=new  double[N][N];

 // constructor
 public SRP_FEM_UnsteadyState (  )
      { 
Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);
        System.out.println("Enter Length(m), of the slab/billet (etc 0.5) ");

 double  L = input.nextDouble();

System.out.println( "Enter the physical & thermal properties of the billet 5 data");

 double  d = input.nextDouble();

     System.out.println("thermal conductivity W/m/deg K.(etc 60)");

 double K = input.nextDouble();

       System.out.println("Specific heat in joule/kg/deg K (etc=500)");

 double  cp = input.nextDouble();

      System.out.println(" Heat transfer coeficient W/m2/deg k(etc 160)");
 double h = input.nextDouble();

     System.out.println("Enter the temperature to be heated to (etc 1400) & ambient temp C(etc 303) ");

 double Tf = input.nextDouble();
 double  Ts = input.nextDouble();

      System.out.println("Enter  total time sec 1100");
 double t  = input.nextInt();

System.out.println("Enter grid length dx in m 0.05 & time step dt in secs 60 ");
 double dx = input.nextDouble();
 double  dt = input.nextDouble();
      T0=303;
      ap=K/(cp*d)   ;

  r=ap*dt/(dx*dx);
tc=0;  
   n=(int)(L/dx);
   m=(int) ( t/dt);
   System.out.println("The material is being heated from left with F/s Temp "+Tf+"k & RHS at ambient temp \n ");
    System.out.println("Temperature with increasing distance "+dx + " m from L to R & Time "+ dt +" secs down \n ");

 for (i=1;i<=n;i++)
   {
T[i]=T0; // at t=0
   }
   nn=n+1;
   mm=m+1;
 do
   {

 for (i=1;i<=n;i++)
   {
 for(j=1;j<=n;j++)
      {
         B[i][j]=0;       
      }        
   }
    B[1][1]=1;
   B[1][2]=-1;
   B[n][n-1]=-1;
    B[n][n]=1;
    j=1;
 for (i=2;i<=n-1; i++)
   {   
   B[i][j]=-1;
    B[i][j+1]=2;
   B[i][j+2]=-1;  
   j=j+1;
      }
for (i=1;i<=n;i++)
   {

 for(j=1;j<=n;j++)
      {
         D[i][j]=0;       
      }        
   }

    D[1][1]=2;
   D[1][2]=1;
   D[n][n-1]=1;
    D[n][n]=2;
    j=1;
 for (i=2;i<=n-1; i++)
   {

   D[i][j]=1;
    D[i][j+1]=4;
   D[i][j+2]=1;  
   j=j+1;
      }
 for (i=1;i<=n;i++)
   {
 for(j=1;j<=n;j++)
      {
         E[i][j]=0;        
      }        
   }

    E[1][1]=2;
   E[1][2]=1;
   E[n][n-1]=1;
    E[n][n]=2;
    j=1;
 for (i=2;i<=n-1; i++)
   {   
   E[i][j]=1;
    E[i][j+1]=4;
   E[i][j+2]=1;  
   j=j+1;
      }

for (i=1;i<=n;i++)
   {
 for(j=1;j<=n;j++)
      {
      E[i][j]=E[i][j]/6;
       }       
   }

// Add r*[B] + 1/6*[D] to make a[i][j] LHS Coefficient of AT=B eqn.

for (i=1;i<=n;i++)
   {
 for(j=1;j<=n;j++)
      {
      a[i][j]=r*B[i][j]+D[i][j]/6;

      }       
   }

//Multiply [E]*{T[i][j]} to form b[i] RHS of of AT=B eqn

 for (i=1;i<=n;i++)
   {
      sum=0.0;
 for(j=1;j<=n;j++)
      {
       sum=sum+E[i][j]*T[i];
      }
         b[i]=sum;
   }

//Boundary conditions at x=0 T=Tf & x=n T=Ts for all t
   a[i][i]=1;

 for(j=2;j<=n;j++)
      {
      a[1][j]=0;
     }

     a[n][n]=1;

for(j=1;j<=n-1;j++)
      {
      a[n][j]=0;
     }
// Determination of boundary Temp
T[1]= T[3]-2*dx*h*(T[2]-Tf)/K;//Left boundary
  T[n]=T[n-2]+Ts*2*dx/K;//Right boundary

   b[1]=T[1];// Left boundary
   b[n]=T[n];//Right boundary

TempMatrix();

for ( i=1; i<=n; i++) // liquid
   {
System.out.print( "\t"+(int)T[i]);

   }
System.out.println();
tc=tc +dt;
   }
 while (tc <=t);

    }  

public  double TempMatrix()
{
 for (i=1;i<=n; i++)
   {
 for(j=1;j<=n;j++)
      {
         al[i][j]=0.0;
         au[i][j]=0.0;
      }
   }
 for(j=1;j<=n;j++)
      {
         au[j][j]=1.0;
         al[j][1]=a[j][1];
         au[1][j]=a[1][j]/al[1][1];
      }

 for(j=2;j<=n;j++)
      {
 for (i=j;i<=n;i++)
         {
            sum =0.0;
 for(k=1;k<=j-1;k++)
            {
               sum=sum+al[i][k]*au[k][j];
            }
               al[i][j]=a[i][j]-sum;
         }

 if(j<=n)
      {
 for(jj=j+1;jj<=n;jj++)
      {
      sum=0.0;
 for(kk=1;kk<=j-1;kk++)
      {
      sum=sum+al[j][kk]*au[kk][jj];
      }
      au[j][jj]=(a[j][jj]-sum)/al[j][j];
      }
      }
      }
         z[1]=b[1]/al[1][1];
 for(j=2;j<=n;j++)
         {
            sum=0.0;
 for(k=1;k<=j-1;k++)
            {
               sum=sum+al[j][k]*z[k];
            }
            z[j]=(b[j]-sum)/al[j][j];
         }
         T[n]=z[n];
 for(j=2;j<=n;j++)
         {
            l=n-j+1;
            sum=0.0;
 for(k=l+1;k<=n;k++)
            {
            sum=sum+au[l][k]*T[k];
            }
           T[l]=z[l]-sum;
         }

 return (0);
}           
 public  static  void main(String args[])
       {

SRP_FEM_UnsteadyState bht = new SRP_FEM_UnsteadyState ( ) ;

     }                         
  }

import java.io.*;
import java.util.Scanner;

public  class SP2_CoolingSlab
{
 public  double  a,lm,dtm,lamda,X,dX,tt,t,tc,Ts,Tb1,Tb2,A,V,T0, L,w,th,d,k,h,e,c;
 int tm ;

 int  dt=(tm/20);// make time strip 5% of total time
 int  mm= (tm/dt);

 double dx= L/20; // make length strip 5% of total length
 int nn=(int)(L/dx);

 double [][]T= new  double [100][100] ;

 // constructor
 public SP2_CoolingSlab ( )
    {
 int  n=nn-1;
 int  m=mm+1;

 double dX=dx/L;
 double  a=k/(c*d);
 double r=a*dt/(dx*dx);

 //Left Boundary Temperature j time i distance
 //double T0;
     T[1][0]=T0;
     T[0][0]=T0;

 //Right Boundary Temperature j time, i distance
 for(int j=1;j<=m;j++)
      {
       T[n+1][j]=Tb2;        
      }

 for (int i=1;i<=n; i++)
     {
       T[i][1]=T0;
      }
// Determination of Temp Distribution
 for(int j=1;j<=m;j++)
      {
        BoundaryTemp(T[1][j-1],T[0][j-1]);// call the fn  boundary temp
         T[0][j]= Tb1;
            lm=a*dt/(dx*dx);

 for (int i=1;i<=n; i++)
        {
           T[i][j+1]=lm*T[i-1][j] + (1-2*lm)*T[i][j] +lm*T[i+1][j];
        }
      }   

 for (int j=1;j<=m;j++)
      {
 for( int i=0;i<=n+1;i++)
          {

          System.out.print(" T"+j+i +T[i][j]);
          }
         System.out.println();
      }                     
    }
 public  double BoundaryTemp(double Ts2,double Ts1)
      {    tt=a*dt/(L*L);//tt dimensioless time
           lamda=tt/(dX*dX);

 if((Ts-Ts1)<=0) Tb1=Ts2;
 else
             Tb1=Ts2 + h*dx/k*(Ts-Ts1);
 return (Tb1);
        }

import java.io.*;
import java.util.Scanner;

public  class SP_BilletHeat
{
 public  double  a,lm,x,dt,dx,dtm,L,tt,lamda,X,dX,tm,t,cp,d,k,tc,Tf,Ts,e,h;  

 double [][]T= new  double [100][100] ;

 // constructor
 public SP_BilletHeat ( )
    {
 double   dX=dx/L;
 double   a=k/(cp*d);
 int  i,j,n,nn,m,mm;

         n=(int)(L/dx);// No of length strips
 doublet=tm*a/(L*L); //Dimensionless Time
         m=(int)(tm/dt);

       nn=n+1;
      mm=m+1;                     
   j=0;// zero time & after ist interval Temperature of the hot body uniform at Tf
//
 for( i=1;i<=nn;i++)
     {

      T[i][j]= Tf;
      T[i][j+1]= Tf;
     }
   j=1;  t=0;

 //"Boundary Temperarure decreasing with time due to cooling by air."<<"\n\n";

 for ( j=1; j<=mm; j++ ) //j=2 at t + dt time
     {
            i=1;
         t=t+dt;
        tt=a*t/(L*L);//tt dimensionless time related to thermal diffusivity
      lamda=tt/(dx*dx);// quantity

// Dtermination oftemp  of  boundary which is in direct contact with surrounding // j time,

    T[i][j]=2*lamda*(T[i+1][j-1]+dX*h*L*(Ts)/k)+ T[i][j-1]*(1- 2*lamda*(1+ dX*h*L/k));

 if(T[i][j]<=Ts) T[i][j]=Ts; // Ts temperature of the surrunding

 //Determination of Temperature distribution along the length after t time period.
 for ( i=1; i<=n+1; i++)
        {
T[0][j]=T[i][j]+30; // Beyond boundary Temperature

        T[i][j+1]= lamda*(T[i+1][j] + T[i-1][j]) + (1-2*lamda)*T[i][j];
 if(T[i][j]<=Ts) T[i][j]=Ts;
 //For next step time t+dt temperature at intermediate point
         }
      }
 // output
 for ( j=1;j<mm;j++)
         {
 for( i=1;i<=nn/2;i++)
           {

          System.out.print(" T"+j+i +T[i][j]);
            }
 for( i=nn/2;i>=1;i--)
           {           
            System.out.print(" T"+j+i +T[i][j]);
            }
             System.out.println();
          }                       
    }

import java.io.*;
import java.util.Scanner;
import java.math.*;

public  class SP_BilletHeat_Implicit
{
 public  double ap,r,lm,dt,dx,dtm,L,tm,t,cp,d,K,h,tc,Tf,Ti,Ts;
 public  double xboundary1,xboundary2,x0, sum;

 double []T= new  double [100] ;
 double [][] a= new  double [40][40]  ;
 double [][] au= new  double [40][40];
 double [][] al= new  double [40][40];
 double []x= new  double [40];
 double []b= new  double [40];
 double []z= new  double [40];
 // constructor
 public SP_BilletHeat_Implicit ( )
    {

 int i,j,n,nn,m,mm,jj,k,kk,l;

  ap=K/(cp*d)   ;
   r=ap*dt/(dx*dx);

   n=(int)(L/dx);
   m=(int)( t/dt);               

       nn=n+1;
       mm=m+1;
 for ( i=1; i<=n; i++) // liquid
   {
T[i]=Ti; // Initially j=1 liquid at Uniform temp from
 // 2nd layer to  last last layer
   }
 for (i=1;i<=n; i++)
   {

    x[i]=T[i];// Initial conc ,i=1 Left end, i=n right end
 // x0=initial conc of the stock
   } 

 for (i=1;i<=n;i++)
   {
 for(j=1;j<=n;j++)
      {
         a[i][j]=0;    
      }        
   }

   a[1][1]=2+2*r;
   a[1][2]=-r;
   a[n][n-1]=-r;
    a[n][n]=2+2*r;
    j=1;
 for (i=2;i<=n-1; i++)
   {

   a[i][j]=-r;
    a[i][j+1]=2+2*r;
   a[i][j+2]=-r;  
   j=j+1;
      }
    tc=0; 
//"The material is being heated from left with F/s Temp "<<Tf<<"k & RHS at ambient temp \n";
 //"Temperature with increasing distance "<<dx <<" m from L to R & Time "<< dt<<" secs down \n"; 

 do
   {
   T[0]= T[2]-2*dx*h*(T[1]-Tf)/K;//Left boundary

   T[n+1]=T[n-1]+Ti*2*dx/K;//Right boundary

   x[0]=T[0];// consider at i=0,x[0]=xboundary1 for all j values ,i e time
   x[n+1]=T[n+1];//consider at i=n+1,x[n+1]=xboundary2 for all j values ,i e time

   b[1]=r*x[0]+(2-2*r)*x[1] + r*x[2] + r*x[0] ;// RHS column 1st row
  b[n]= r*x[n-1]+(2-2*r)*x[n]+ r*x[n+1] + r*x[n+1];// RHS column last row

 for (i=2;i<=n-1; i++)
     {
        b[i]= r*x[i-1]+(2-2*r)*x[i] + r*x[i+1] ;
      } 

 // Solution of the Matrix

 for (i=1;i<=n; i++)
   {
 for(j=1;j<=n;j++)
      {
         al[i][j]=0.0;
         au[i][j]=0.0;
      }
   }
 for(j=1;j<=n;j++)
      {
         au[j][j]=1.0;
         al[j][1]=a[j][1];
         au[1][j]=a[1][j]/al[1][1];
      }
 for(j=2;j<=n;j++)
      {
 for (i=j;i<=n;i++)
         {
            sum =0.0;
 for(k=1;k<=j-1;k++)
            {
               sum=sum+al[i][k]*au[k][j];
            }
               al[i][j]=a[i][j]-sum;
         }

 if(j<=n)
      {
 for(jj=j+1;jj<=n;jj++)
      {
      sum=0.0;
 for(kk=1;kk<=j-1;kk++)
      {
      sum=sum+al[j][kk]*au[kk][jj];
      }
      au[j][jj]=(a[j][jj]-sum)/al[j][j];
      }
      }
      }

         z[1]=b[1]/al[1][1];
 for(j=2;j<=n;j++)
         {
            sum=0.0;
 for(k=1;k<=j-1;k++)
            {
               sum=sum+al[j][k]*z[k];
            }
            z[j]=(b[j]-sum)/al[j][j];
         }

         x[n]=z[n];
 for(j=2;j<=n;j++)
         {
            l=n-j+1;
            sum=0.0;
 for(k=l+1;k<=n;k++)
            {
            sum=sum+au[l][k]*x[k];
            }
           x[l]=z[l]-sum;
         }

   tc=tc +dt;
 for (i=1;i<=n; i++)
      {

       System.out.print(" " +x[i]);

      }
 for ( i=1; i<=n; i++) // liquid
     {
T[i]=x[i]; // Initially j=1 liquid at Uniform temp from
 // 2nd layer to  last last layer
     }

   }
 while (tc <=t);

  }       
 public  static  void main(String args[])
       {
 int n,m  ;
Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);

      System.out.print(

"Enter Length(m),width(m), thicknesss(m)of the slab/billet ");

 double  L = input.nextDouble();
 double  w = input.nextDouble();
 double th = input.nextDouble();

     System.out.print(

 "Enter the temperature to be heated to & ambient temp C ");

 double Tf = input.nextDouble();
 double  Ts = input.nextDouble();
      System.out.println(

"Enter the physical & thermal properties of the billet 5 data");
      System.out.print(

"Density Kg/m3,thermal conductivity W/m/deg.K,Sp heat joule/kg/deg K, heat transfer coefficientW/m2/k ");

 double  d = input.nextDouble();
 double k = input.nextDouble();
 double  cp = input.nextDouble();
 double h = input.nextDouble();

        System.out.print("Enter  total time sec");
 int tm = input.nextInt();

        System.out.print("Enter grid length dx & time step dt ");
 double dx = input.nextDouble();
 double  dt = input.nextDouble();

SP_BilletHeat_Implicit bhim= new SP_BilletHeat_Implicit() ;
      }                         
  }

Program 8.5.1 SP_Solidification.java 

import java.io.*;
import java.util.Scanner;
import java.math.*;

public  class SP_Solidification
{
 public  double  a,lm,x,dt,dx,dtm,L,tt,Tli,Tm,lamda,X,dX,tm,t,cp,d,k,tc,Tf,Ts,e,h;

 double [][]T= new  double [100][100] ;
 double [][]Y= new  double [50][50] ;
 double []Time= new  double [50] ;
 // constructor
 public SP_Solidification ( )
    {
 int  i,j,n,nn,m,mm;
          dX=dx/L;  a=k/(cp*d)   ;

   n=(int)(L/dx);// No of length strips
t=tm*a/(L*L); //Dimensionless Time

     m=(int)(tm/dt) ;    
      nn=n+1;
      mm=m+1;                     

 for ( i=2; i<=n+1; i++) // liquid
   {
T[i][1]=1200; // Initially j=1 liquid at Uniform temp from
 // 2nd layer to  last last layer
   }
 for( j=1; j<=m; j++ )
   {
T[n+1][j]=1200; // At y=infinity i=n+1 Temp remain same
   }
 for( j=1; j<=m; j++ )
    {
T[1][j]=303; // 1st layer of liquid in contact t
    }                

// with solid is below freezing poin due to supercooling

 for ( j=1; j<=m; j++ ) //j=1 at t=0 time
   {       
 for ( i=2; i<=n; i++) // i=1 ,interface/boundary,
      {
           T[i][j+1]= T[i][j] + ((a*dt)/(dx*dx))*(T[i-1][j] +T[i+1][j]  -2*T[i][j]);
       }
    }

  System.out.println(

"Temperature Distribution for cooling of Liquid from Left to right at an interval of " +dx );
  System.out.println(" time interval secc" +dt);

 for( j=1;j<=m;j++)
   {
 for( i=2;i<=n;i++)
      {
 if(T[i][j]<=Tm)
         {
 //" Solid";
Y[i][j]=(i-1)*dx; // i=2 at distance of dx  from boundary
          Time[j]=(j-1)*dt;
         }
 else
        {  
        System.out.print(" " +T[i][j] );
        }
     }

   }
 // output
     System.out.println("Solidified skin from L to R with time sec" +dt);

 for( j=1;j<=m;j++)
      {
 for( i=2;i<=n;i++)
{ if(Y[i][j]!=0)
 if(Y[i][j]!=Y[i-1][j] && Y[i][j]!=Y[i][j-1]) // Y[i][j]!=0)
             {
               System.out.print(" " +Y[i][j] +Time[j]);
               System.out.println();
             }
          }
       }

    }   
 public  static  void main(String args[])
       {
 int n,m  ;
Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);

      System.out.print("Enter Length(m),width(m), thicknesss(m)of the slab/billet ");

 double  L = input.nextDouble();
 double  w = input.nextDouble();
 double th = input.nextDouble();

     System.out.print("Enter the temperature to be heated to & ambient temp C ");

 double Tf = input.nextDouble();
 double  Ts = input.nextDouble();
System.out.println( "Enter the physical & thermal properties of the billet 5 data");
      System.out.print("Density Kg/m3,thermal conductivity W/m/deg.K,Sp heat joule/kg/deg K, heat transfer coefficientW/m2/k ");

 double  d = input.nextDouble();
 double k = input.nextDouble();
 double  cp = input.nextDouble();
 double h = input.nextDouble();

        System.out.print("Enter  total time sec");
 int tm = input.nextInt();

        System.out.print("Enter grid length dx & time step dt ");
 double dx = input.nextDouble();
 double  dt = input.nextDouble();

SP_Solidification sofn = new SP_Solidification() ;
      }                         
  }        

program 8.6.1 SP_Solidification2.java

import java.io.*;
import java.util.Scanner;
import java.math.*;

public  class SP_Solidification2
{
 public  double  a,lm,x,dt,dx,dtm,L,H,Ys,Yl,dY,tt;
 public  double Tsi,Tli,Tm,tm,t,cp,d,ks,kl,Tf,e,h;
 public  int i,j,n,nn,m,mm;

 double [][]TL= new  double [100][100] ;
 double [][]TS= new  double [100][100] ;
 double []Tl= new  double [50] ;
 double []Ts= new  double [50] ;
 double []Y= new  double [50] ;
 double []Time= new  double [50] ;
 // constructor
 public SP_Solidification2 ( )
    {
 int  i,j,n,nn,m,mm;

 //The data for steel      
    Ys=0.5;Yl=0.5;Tli=1600;Tsi=800;tm=2400;Tm=1800;d=7600;kl=10;ks=30;cp=500;h=360;dx=0.1;dt=120;H=281200;

         Y[0]=0;
        m=(int)( tm/dt);
        n=(int)(L/dx);

         liquidTemp();
          solidTemp();

    System.out.println("Temperature Distribution liquid and solid" +dx );

 for ( j=1;j<=m; j++)
    {
    System.out.println("After secondsds"+j*dt);
 for( i=1; i<=n+1; i++)
       {
      System.out.print(" "+TL[n+2-i][j+1]);
System.out.println ( " "+TS[i][j+1]);
       }
     }

 for ( j=1;j<=m; j++)
   {
     Tl[j]=TL[3][j+1];
      Ts[j]=TS[3][j+1];
 //Convective heat flux through liquid - Conductive heat flux through solid
 //=Heat evolution due to solified skin formed
 //h*(Tl[j]-Tm) -k*(Ts[j]-Tm)=d*H*dY/dt; Units watt/m2 LHS= RHS

        dY=(h*(Tl[j]-Tm) -ks*(Ts[j]-Tm)/Ys)*dt/(d*H) ;

 // for conductive heat transfer through both liquid & solid
 //  dY=(kl*(Tl[j]-Tm)/Yl -ks*(Ts[j]-Tm)/Ys)*dt/(d*H) ;

        Y[j]=Y[j-1]+ dY;

       System.out.print(" "+dt);
       System.out.println(" "+Y[j]);

       Ys=Ys+dY;

// Thickness of solid increases due to newly formed solid dY
         Yl=Yl-dY;

//Thickness of liquid decreases due to newly formed solid dY
        dt=dt + 60;
   }

}               

 public  double liquidTemp()
  {
 /*Ti= Initial temp of liquid
   Tb= Temperature of the boundary awayb from the liquid/solid interface*/
 double  dx,L,k,h,Ti,Tb;

    L=0.5;d=7200;k=40;cp=600;h=60;dx=0.1; Ti=1200;Tb=1200;

       a=k/(cp*d)   ;
 // a= 1.5*pow(10,(-4)) ;
 double r=a*dt/(dx*dx);// For accuracy r shold be <=0.5

      n=(int)(L/dx);// No of length strips
      nn=n+1;
      mm=m+1;

 for ( i=2; i<=n+1; i++) // liquid
   {
TL[i][1]=Ti; // Initially j=1 liquid at Uniform temp from
 // 2nd layer to  last last layer (n+1)
   }

 for( j=1; j<=m; j++ )
   {
TL[n+1][j]=Tb; // At y=infinity i=n+1 Temp remain same
   }

 for( j=1; j<=m; j++ )
    {
TL[1][j]=Tm; // 1st layer of liquid in contact t
    }               

 // with solid is below freezing poin due to supercooling

 for ( j=1; j<=m; j++ ) //j=1 at t=0 time
      {
 for ( i=2; i<=n; i++) // i=1 ,interface/boundary,
            {

             TL[i][j+1]= TL[i][j] + ((a*dt)/(dx*dx))*(TL[i-1][j] +TL[i+1][j]  -2*TL[i][j]);
            }
       }
 return(0);
  }

 public  double solidTemp()
  {
 /*Ti= Initial temp of liquid
   Tb= Temperature of the boundary away from the liquid/solid interface*/
 double  dx,L,k,h,Ti,Tb;

   L=.5;d=7600;k=60;cp=500;h=60;dx=0.1; Ti=400;Tb=400;

    a=k/(cp*d)   ;
 double r=a*dt/(dx*dx);

   n=(int)(L/dx);// No of length strips          
    nn=n+1;
    mm=m+1;

 for ( i=2; i<=n+1; i++) // liquid
   {
TS[i][1]=400; // Initially j=1 liquid at Uniform temp from
   }

 for( j=1; j<=m; j++ )
   {
TS[n+1][j]=400; // At y=infinity i=n+1 Temp remain same
   }

 for( j=1; j<=m; j++ )
    {
TS[1][j]=Tm; // 1st layer of liquid in contact t
    }                

// with solid is below freezing poin due to supercooling

 for ( j=1; j<=m; j++ ) //j=1 at t=0 time
    {
 for ( i=2; i<=n; i++) // i=1 ,interface/boundary,
      {       

    TS[i][j+1]= TS[i][j] + ((a*dt)/(dx*dx))*(TS[i-1][j] +TS[i+1][j]-                            

        2*TS[i][j]);    
      }
    }
 return(0);
  }

import java.io.*;
import java.util.Scanner;
import java.math.*;

public  class SP_ConCast
{
 public  double  a,lm,x,dt,dx,dtm,L,H,Ys,Yl,dY,Ti,Tb,u,tt,dy;
 public  double Tsi,Tli,Tm,tm,t,cp,d,ks,kl,Tf,e,h;
 public  int i,j,n,nn,m,mm,k,p;

 double [][][]T= new  double [50][50][50] ;
 double [][]TL= new  double [100][100] ;
 double [][]Ts= new  double [50][50] ;
 double [][]Y= new  double [50][50] ;

 double []Tl= new  double [50] ;
 double []Time= new  double[50] ; 

 // constructor
 public SP_ConCast ( )
    {
 int  i,j,n,nn,m,mm;

      Ys=0.5; d=7600;ks=60;cp=500;h=60;dx=0.1;tm=600; dt=60; Ti=400;Tb=400; Tm=1800;dy=0.1;
      u=0.01;H=281700;

         m=(int)( tm/dt);
        n=(int)(L/dx);
 double r=a*dt/(dx*dx);
          nn=n+1;
         mm=m+1;
       k=1;
 for ( i=2; i<=n+1; i++) // liquid
   {
T[i][1][k]=Tm; // Initially j=1 liquid at Uniform temp from
 // 2nd layer to  last last layer (n+1)
   }

 for( j=1; j<=m; j++ )
   {
T[n+1][j][k]=Ti; // At y=infinity i=n+1 Temp remain same
   }

 for( j=1; j<=m+1; j++ )
    {
T[1][j][k]=Tm; // 1st layer of liquid in contact t
    }

    System.out.println("Temp Distribution of the 1st vertical section k=1" );

 for(j=1;j<=m+1;j++)
        {

 for(i=2;i<=n;i++)
           {
              T[i][j+1][k]= T[i][j][k] + ((a*dt)/(dx*dx))*(T[i-1][j][k] +T[i+1][j][k]  -2*T[i][j][k]);
 //  if(T[i][j+1][k]<=303) T[i][j+1][k]=303;
            System.out.print(" "+T[i][j+1][k]);
            }
System.out.println( " ");   
         }
     System.out.println("Temperature Distribution from L to R in x-direction & down with increase in time"+dt );

 for ( k=1; k<=10; k++ )
   {
 for ( j=1; j<=m; j++ )
      {
 for ( i=2; i<=n; i++)
         {

            T[i][j][k+1]=T[i][j][k]+(T[i][j+1][k]- T[i][j][k])*dy/(u*dt);
           System.out.println(" "+T[i][j][k+1]);

           }
System.out.println( " ");
         }
         System.out.println("Downwards y direction for next steps" +k);
    }

    System.out.println("Average temperature at a any horizontal section x direction assumed to be near central line");
    System.out.println("Temperature Distribution from Top to down with increase in y for a rate m/sec"+u);
    System.out.println("next column onwards With  Time of  secs"+dt);

 for ( k=1; k<=10; k++ )
   {
 for (j=1; j<=m; j++ )

      {
          System.out.println (" "+T[4][j][k+1]);
      }
    }      
   Y[2][1]=0.0;

 for(j=1; j<=m; j++ )
    {
 for ( k=1; k<=10; k++ )
       {

        dY= -ks*dy*(T[4][j][k+1]-Tm)/(d*H*Ys) ;

        Y[j][k+1]=Y[j][k] +dY;

          System.out.println (" "+dY*100);
          System.out.println (" "+Y[j][k+1]*100);

          Ys=Ys+dY;

// Thickness of solid increases due to newly formed solid dY
          dy=dy+0.1;
        }

         System.out.println("Solidified skin thickness increases after secs "+dt);
      }
    }

import java.io.*;
import java.util.Scanner;
import java.math.*;

public  class SP_ReductionKinetics
{
 public  double  D,lm,x,dt,dy,dtm,L,H,dY,tt;
 public  double C0,Ce,tm,t,d,cm,Cb,N,r;

 public  int i,j,n,nn,m,mm,k;

 double [][]F= new  double [50][50] ;
 double [][]C= new  double [100][100] ;
 double []Y= new  double [50] ;
 double []Time= new  double [50] ;

 // constructor
 public SP_ReductionKinetics( )
    {
 // int  i,j,n,nn,m,mm;

   L=0.5;Ce=0.175;tm=1200;d=7.8;dy=0.1;dt=60;D=0.000071; N=3/2;cm=7.8/56;C0=1;

   r=D*dt/(dy*dy);
   Y[0]=0;
   m=(int)( tm/dt);
   n=(int)(L/dy);// No of length strips
double rr=D*dt*N/cm;

    nn=n+1;
    mm=m+1;

for( j=1; j<=m; j++ )
    {
C[1][j]=C0; // C0=conc of the reacting gas at y=0
    }                

 for ( i=2; i<=n+1; i++) // liquid
   {
C[i][1]=0; // Initially j=1 coc of the reacting gas =0
   }

    System.out.println("Concentration(m) of reducing gas with progres of reduction  at layers " +dy );
    System.out.println("Time interval secs " +dt );

 for ( j=1; j<=m; j++ ) //j=1 at t=0 time
   {
 for ( i=2; i<=n; i++) // i=1 ,interface/boundary,
         {

             C[i][j+1]= C[i][j] + ((D*dt)/(dy*dy))*(C[i-1][j] +C[i+1][j]  -2*C[i][j]);
System.out.println( "  " +C[i][j+1]) ;

 if(C[i][j+1]>Ce)
               {//(N*cm)*dY/dt=D*dc/dY  Material balance
 // cm=molar density of solid product= Density/Mol.wt.
 //N=no of moles of gas reacting permole of solid product

                 dY=Math.pow((D*dt*(C[i][j+1]-Ce)/(N*cm)),0.5) ;
               }
 else dY=0;

            F[i][j]=dY ;
          }
    }

     System.out.println ("Results displaying thickness (cm) of product formed with progres of reduction at layers "+dy);
     System.out.println ("secs time interval down"+dt);

 for ( j=1; j<=m; j++ )
        {
 for ( i=2; i<=n; i++)
          {
System.out.println( "  "+F[i][j]);
           }    
        }    
   }                                   

 public  static  void main(String args[])
       {
 int n,m  ;
Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);

      System.out.print("Enter Length(m),width(m) ");

 double  L = input.nextDouble();

     System.out.print("Enter the temperature to be heated to & ambient temp C ");

 double Tf = input.nextDouble();
 double  Ts = input.nextDouble();
System.out.println( "Enter the physical & thermal properties of the billet 5 data");

 double  d = input.nextDouble();

        System.out.print("Enter  total time sec");
 int tm = input.nextInt();

        System.out.print("Enter grid length dx & time step dt ");
 double dx = input.nextDouble();
 double  dt = input.nextDouble();

SP_ReductionKinetics rdn = new SP_ReductionKinetics() ;
      }                         
  }      

Program 9.2.1  SP_Carburization.java

import java.io.*;
import java.util.Scanner;
import java.math.*;

public  class SP_Carburization
{
 public  int i,j,n,jj,k,kk,l,iter;
 public  double xboundary1,xboundary2,L,dx,dt,x0,t,d,tc,r, sum;
 double [][] a= new  double [40][40]  ;
 double [][] au= new  double [40][40];
 double [][] al= new  double [40][40];
 double []x= new  double [40];
 double []b= new  double [40];
 double []z= new  double [40];

 // constructor
 public SP_Carburization ( )
    {

      L=1.0; dx=0.2; xboundary1=0.2;   xboundary2=1.3 ;x0=0.2; d=0.00002;  dt=60; t=600;

     r=d*dt/(dx*dx);  
     n=(int)(L/dx);            

 for (i=1;i<=n; i++)
   {
   x[i]=x0;//Initial conc ,i=1 Left end, i=n right end
 // x0=initial conc of the stock
} // i=0 beyond left end ,i=n+1 beyond right end fictious points

 do
   {
       x[0]=xboundary1;

// consider at i=0,x[0]=xboundary1 for all j values ,i e time
       x[n+1]=xboundary2;

//consider at i=n+1,x[n+1]=xboundary2 for all j values ,i e time

      b[1]=r*x[0]+(2-2*r)*x[1] + r*x[2] + r*xboundary1 ;

// RHS column 1st row
      b[n]= r*x[n-1]+(2-2*r)*x[n]+ r*x[n+1] + r*xboundary2;

// RHS column last row

 for (i=2;i<=n-1; i++)
      {
       b[i]= r*x[i-1]+(2-2*r)*x[i] + r*x[i+1] ;
       }

 for (i=1;i<=n;i++)
     {
 for(j=1;j<=n;j++)
         {
         a[i][j]=0;       
         }        
       }

        a[1][1]=2+2*r;
        a[1][2]=-r;
        a[n][n-1]=-r;
         a[n][n]=2+2*r;

    j=1;
 for (i=2;i<=n-1; i++)
    {
      a[i][j]=-r;
       a[i][j+1]=2+2*r;
       a[i][j+2]=-r;  
       j=j+1;
      }   
 // Solution of Matrix

 for (i=1;i<=n; i++)
   {
 for(j=1;j<=n;j++)
      {
         al[i][j]=0.0;
         au[i][j]=0.0;
      }
   }
 for(j=1;j<=n;j++)
      {
         au[j][j]=1.0;
         al[j][1]=a[j][1];
         au[1][j]=a[1][j]/al[1][1];
      }
 for(j=2;j<=n;j++)
      {
 for (i=j;i<=n;i++)
         {
            sum =0.0;
 for(k=1;k<=j-1;k++)
            {
               sum=sum+al[i][k]*au[k][j];
            }
               al[i][j]=a[i][j]-sum;
          }

 if(j<=n)
         {
 for(jj=j+1;jj<=n;jj++)
            {
             sum=0.0;
 for(kk=1;kk<=j-1;kk++)
               {
                sum=sum+al[j][kk]*au[kk][jj];
                }
               au[j][jj]=(a[j][jj]-sum)/al[j][j];
             }
          }
        }                  

 //solution of alZ=B using forward substitution

         z[1]=b[1]/al[1][1];
 for(j=2;j<=n;j++)
         {
            sum=0.0;
 for(k=1;k<=j-1;k++)
            {
               sum=sum+al[j][k]*z[k];
            }
            z[j]=(b[j]-sum)/al[j][j];
         }

         x[n]=z[n];

 for(j=2;j<=n;j++)
         {
            l=n-j+1;
            sum=0.0;
 for(k=l+1;k<=n;k++)
            {
            sum=sum+au[l][k]*x[k];
            }
           x[l]=z[l]-sum;
         }

      tc=tc +dt;
      System.out.println(" " +tc);

 for (i=1;i<=n; i++)
      {
        System.out.println(" " +x[i]);
      }
   }
 while (tc<=t);

}  
 public  static  void main(String args[])
       {
 int n,m  ;
Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);

      System.out.print("Enter Length(m) ");

 double  L = input.nextDouble();

     System.out.print("concentration at x =0 & t=t and at x =L & t=t ");

 double xboundary1 = input.nextDouble();
 double  xboundary2 = input.nextDouble();

System.out.println( "Initial concentration at x =x & t=0");
 double  x0 = input.nextDouble();

        System.out.print("Enter  total time sec");
 int t = input.nextInt();

        System.out.print("Diffusivity cm2/min=");
 int d = input.nextInt();

        System.out.print("Enter grid length dx & time step dt ");
 double dx = input.nextDouble();
 double  dt = input.nextDouble();

SP_Carburization crb= new SP_Carburization() ;

      }                         
  }      

Program  9.3.1 SP_MaterialBalanace.java

import java.io.*;
import java.util.Scanner;
import java.math.*;

public  class SP_MaterialBalance
{
 public  int i,j;
 public  double a1,a2,a3,a4,a5,b1,b2,b3,b4,b5,c1,c2,c3,c4,c5,P,Q,R,S;

 double [][] A= new  double [10][10];
 double [][] B= new  double [10][10];
 double [][] Y= new  double [10][10];
 double [][] L= new  double [10][10];
 double [][] U= new  double [10][10];
 double [][] X= new  double [10][10];

 // constructor
 public SP_MaterialBalance ( )
    {          
       A[0][0]=(a1/100);
     A[0][1]=(a2/100);
      A[0][2]=(a3/100);                                                                      
      A[1][0]=(b1/100);
     A[1][1]=(b2/100);
      A[1][2]=((b3/100)-(S/(100*R)));
     A[2][0]=(c1/100);
     A[2][1]=(c2/100);
     A[2][2]=(c3/100);

     B[0][0]=((P*a4+Q*a5)/100);
     B[1][0]=(((P*b4*(60/28)+Q*b5))/100);
      B[2][0]=((P*c4+Q*c5)/100);

 for(i=0;i<3;i++)
  {
 for(j=1;j<3;j++)

   {
      B[i][j]=X[i][j]=Y[i][j]=0;
   }
  }

L[0][0]=L[1][1]=L[2][2]=1;
L[0][1]=L[0][2]=L[1][2]=U[1][0]=U[2][0]=U[2][1]=0;

U[0][0]=A[0][0];
U[0][1]=A[0][1];
U[0][2]=A[0][2];
 /*LU Decomposition*/
L[1][0]=A[1][0]/U[0][0];
U[1][1]=(A[1][1]-L[1][0]*U[0][1]);
U[1][2]=(A[1][2]-L[1][0]*U[0][2]); /*splitting into L & U*/
L[2][0]=A[2][0]/U[0][0];
L[2][1]=(A[2][1]-L[2][0]*U[0][1])/U[1][1];

U[2][2]=(A[2][2]-((L[2][0]*U[0][2])+(L[2][1]*U[1][2])));

Y[0][0]=B[0][0]/L[0][0];
Y[1][0]=(B[1][0]-(L[1][0]*Y[0][0]));
Y[2][0]=(B[2][0]-((L[2][0]*Y[0][0])+(L[2][1]*Y[1][0]))); /*forward substitution*/

X[2][0]=Y[2][0]/U[2][2];
X[1][0]=((Y[1][0]-(U[1][2]*X[2][0]))/U[1][1]);
X[0][0]=((Y[0][0]-((U[0][1]*X[1][0])+(U[0][2]*X[2][0])))/U[0][0]);/*backward substitution*/

System.out.println (

"Iron ore to be charged,Coke to be charged,Limestone to be charged");

System.out.println ("  " +X[0][0]);
System.out.print ("  " +X[1][0]);
System.out.println ("  " +X[2][0]);

    }  
 public  static  void main(String args[])
       {
 int n,m  ;
Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);

      System.out.print(

"pecentage of Fe in iron ore,coke,limestone,hot metal,off gas");

 double  a1 = input.nextDouble();
 double  a2 = input.nextDouble();
 double  a3 = input.nextDouble();
 double  a4 = input.nextDouble();
 double  a5 = input.nextDouble();

System.out.print(

"pecentage of Silica in iron ore,coke,limestone,hot metal,off gas");

 double  b1 = input.nextDouble();
 double  b2 = input.nextDouble();
 double  b3 = input.nextDouble();
 double  b4 = input.nextDouble();
 double  b5 = input.nextDouble();

   System.out.print(

"pecentage of Carbon in iron ore,coke,limestone,hot metal,off gas");

 double  c1 = input.nextDouble();
 double  c2 = input.nextDouble();
 double  c3 = input.nextDouble();
 double  c4 = input.nextDouble();
 double  c5 = input.nextDouble();

System.out.print(

"The amount of hot metal to be produced(in tons per day)");

 double  P = input.nextDouble();

System.out.print(

"The amount of off gas produced(in tons per day):");

 double  Q = input.nextDouble();

  System.out.print("percentage of lime in limestone charged ");

 double  S = input.nextDouble();

    System.out.print("The basicity of the limestone charged");

 double  R = input.nextDouble();

SP_MaterialBalance mat= new SP_MaterialBalance() ;
     }                         
  }

public  void slag()
{
cal1= xhm1*whm;
cal2=xls1*wls;
cal3=wsl*7/9;
cal4=cal1-cal2;
cal5=cal4/cal3;

cal6= xhm2*whm;
cal7=xls2*wls;
cal8=wsl*27/35;
cal9=cal6-cal7;
cal10=cal9/cal8;

cal11= xhm3*whm;
cal12=xls3*wls;
cal13=wsl*7/15;
cal14=cal11-cal12;
cal15=cal14/cal13;

cal16= xhm4*whm;
cal17=xls4*wls;
cal18=wsl*31/142;
cal19=cal16-cal17;
cal20=cal19/cal18;

cal21= xhm5*whm;
cal22=xls5*wls;
cal23=wsl;
cal24=cal21-cal22;
cal25=cal24/cal23;

xsl2=cal10;xsl3=cal5;xsl4=cal20;xsl5=cal25;xsl6=cal15;

}
public  double heatIn()
{
/*1) Sensible heat of hot metal*/

 if (t1>1450)
    {shm= 0.01*(t1-1450);}
 else
   { shm1=0;
shm2 = shm1 + 12.59;
shm3 = whm*xhm1*10000/56;
shm = shm3 * shm2;}

/*2) Oxidation heat of Fe */
t1=t1+273;
t2=t2+273;

hfe1= 64.5;
hfe2=12.38*t2;
hfe3=1.62*0.5*0.001*t2*t2;
hfe4=0.38*100000/t2;
hfe5=hfe2+hfe3+hfe4-7705.43;
hfe6=hfe5/1000;
hfe7=hfe1-hfe6;
hfe8=xsl3*10000*wsl/72;
hfe9=hfe8*hfe7;

/*3) Oxidation heat of Si*/

hsi1= 210.2;
hsi2= 14.4*t2;
hsi3= 2.04*0.5*0.001*t2*t2;
hsi4= 0*100000/t2;
hsi5= hsi2+hsi3+hsi4-4381.78;
hsi6= hsi5/1000;
hsi7= hsi1-hsi6;
hsi8= xsl6*10000*wsl/60;
hsi9= hsi8*hsi7;

/*4) Oxidation heat of Mn*/

hmn1= 92.0;
hmn2= 11.11*t2;
hmn3= 1.94*0.5*0.001*t2*t2;
hmn4= 0.88*100000/t2;
hmn5= hmn2+hmn3+hmn4-3524.42;
hmn6= hmn5/1000;
hmn7= hmn1-hmn6;
hmn8= xsl2*10000*wsl/70;
hmn9= hmn8*hmn7;

/*5) Oxidation heat of P*/

hop1= 370.0;
hop2= 8.37*t2;
hop3= 54.0*0.5*0.001*t2*t2;
hop4= 0*100000/t2;
hop5= hop2+hop3+hop4-4891.96;
hop6= hop5/1000;
hop7= hop1-hop6;
hop8= xsl4*10000*wsl/142;
hop9= hop8*hop7;

/*6) Oxidation heat of C*/
hoc1= 26.4;
hoc2= 6.79*t2;
hoc3= 0.98*0.5*0.001*t2*t2;
hoc4= 0.11*100000/t2;
hoc5= hoc2+hoc3+hoc4-2103.84;
hoc6= hoc5/1000;
hoc7= hoc1-hoc6;
hoc8= xls6*wls;
hoc9= xhm6*whm;
hoc10= hoc9-hoc8;
hoc11= hoc10*1000000/12;
hoc12= hoc11*hoc7;

calin = shm + hfe9 + hsi9 + hop9 + hoc12 + hmn9  ;
return (calin);
}

/*1) Sensible heat of liquid steel*/
dt=t2-t1;
sls1 = 0.01 * dt;
sls2 = 12.59+sls1;
sls3 = wls*xls1 *10000/56;
sls = sls2 *sls3;

/*2) Sensible heat of slag*/
  hla1 = 36.51*298;

  hlm3 = 6.16*100000/298;
  hlm4 = hlm1 + hlm2 + hlm3;
  hlm5 = 26.42*t2;
  hlm6 = 3.88*0.5*0.001*t2*t2;
  hlm7 = 6.16*100000/t2;
  hlm8 = hlm5 + hlm6 + hlm7;
  hlm9 = hlm8 - hlm4;
  hlm10= hlm9/1000;  hlm10 = hlm10 + 5.9 ;
  hlm11= xsl2 *10000*wsl/70;
  hlm12= hlm10 *hlm11;

  hps1 = 48.24*298;
  hps2 = 39.68*0.5*0.001*298*298;
  hps3 = 5.00*100000/298;
  hps4 = hps1 + hps2 + hps3;
  hps5 = 48.24*t2;
  hps6 = 39.68*0.5*0.001*t2*t2;
  hps7 = 5.00*100000/t2;
  hps8 = hps5 + hps6 + hps7;
  hps9 = hps8 - hps4;

  hps11= xsl4 *10000*wsl/142;

  hcs3= xsl3 *10000*wsl*0.5/72;
  hcs4= hcs1-hcs2-hcs3;
  hcs5 = 36.25*298;
  hcs6 = 8.83*0.5*0.001*298*298;
  hcs7 = 7.24*100000/298;
  hcs8 = hcs5 + hcs6 + hcs7 ;
  hcs9 = 36.25*t2;
  hcs10= 8.83*0.5*0.001*t2*t2;
  hcs11= 7.24*100000/t2;
  hcs12= hcs9 + hcs10 + hcs11;
  hcs13= hcs12 - hcs8;
  hcs14= hcs13/1000; hcs14 =hcs14 + 30.2;
  hcs15= hcs14*hcs4;

ssla=  hla12 + hlm12 + hps12 + hcs15 ;

/*3) Heating of lime to 1700*/
    t= 298;
  hli1 = 11.86 *t;
  hli2 = 1.08* 0.5*0.001*t*t;
  hli3 = 1.66 *100000/t;
  hli4 = hli1 +hli2 +hli3;

  hli5 = 11.86 *t2;
  hli6 = 1.08*0.5*0.001*t2*t2;
  hli7 = 1.66*100000/t2;
  hli8 = hli5 + hli6 +hli7;

  hli9 = hli8 -hli4;
  hli10 = hli9/10000;
  hli11 = xsl1*wsl*10000/56;
  hli = hli10*hli11;

 /*4) sensible heat of LD gas*/
    t3=t3+273;

   hg1= xls6*wls;
  hg2= xhm6*whm;
  hg3= hg2-hg1;
  hg4= hg3*1000000/12;
  hg5= t3-298;
  hg6=t3*t3;
  hg7=298*298;
  hg8=hg6-hg7;
  hg9=1/298;
  hg10=1/t3;
  hg11=hg10-hg9;
  hg12=6.79*hg5;
  hg13=0.98*0.5*0.001*hg8;
  hg14=.11*hg11;
  hg15=hg12+hg13+hg14;
  hg16=10.55*hg5;
  hg17=2.16*0.5*0.001*hg8;
  hg18=2.04*hg11;
  hg19=hg16+hg17+hg18;
  hg20=hg15+hg19;
  hg21=hg20/1000;
  sld=hg4*hg21;

  calout= sls + ssla + sld + hli;
 return (calout);
}

// Genetic alogorithin to find Max corrosion with combination of Cl,SO4 & pH  .

import java.io.*;
import java.util.Scanner;

// geneLength 6 max no 111111=63, 8- 11111111=255 10-1111111111=1023, 12- 4095

public  class SP_GeneticCorrosion
{

String []g= new String [100];

 double []fitness= new  double [500];

 double mutation_rate=0.001;
 public  float target;

 //  int population=40 ;
 int chromLength=18;
 int geneLength=6;
 //  int geneLength=8;
 int Max_No_Generation=200;

 public  boolean solutionFound;
 // public float target;

 // constructor
 public SP_GeneticCorrosion( )
    {
 //  Generate chromosome population

 double target = input.nextDouble();

 //    srand(1);// random number seed or starter
 // System.out.print("\nGenerating Random fraction double \n");

 for(j=1;j<=population; j++)
  {

c[j] = "";
 int temp = j;
 for (i=1; i<=chromLength;i++)
            {
Random rn = new Random();

 int rnd = rn.nextInt(1000);

 double  rf=(double)rnd/1000;

 if (rf<=0.5)
 // if (temp%2 == 1)
c[j] = '1'+c[j];
 else
c[j] = '0'+c[j];
 //  temp = temp/2;
            }
 //  System.out.println(c[j]); 

    } 

//    c[1]="01101"; c[2]="1100"; c[3]="0101"; c[4]="1110"; c[5]="0011";

 int No_of_genration=1;
solutionFound=false;

while (!solutionFound)// Loop will continue till solutionFound
{ // is not false i e true i e solution found

 // Initially make all population fitness=0

 for(j=1;j<=population; j++)
   {
      fitness[j] =0.0;
 // System.out.print(<" fitness= "+fitness[j]);

   }
System.out.println();
// find fitness from chromo value
 for(j=1;j<=population; j++)

//System.out.print( " chromo_Value( c[j])= "+chromo_Value( c[j])) ;

chromo_fitness(chromoValue[j]);
  System.out.println();
System.out.println( " chromo_fitness(chromoValue[j]= "+chromo_fitness(chromoValue[j]));

//Test if any chromosome has achieved required fitness

 if(fitness[j]>=0.01 )
{

   System.out.println("No_of_population "+population);

   System.out.println("fitness= "+fitness[j]);

    System.out.println("Gene combiations are");

 for(i=1;i<=(chromLength/geneLength);i++)
  {
    System.out.println(" No="+i+" Gene (Binary)"+g[i]+"="+gDecimal[i]);
   }
  System.out.println();

    solutionFound=true;
 break;

  }

   }

float totalfitness=0;
for(j=1;j<=population; j++)
   {

     totalfitness +=fitness[j];

   }

String offspring1;
String offspring2;
int pop =1;
  j=1;

 do {

offspring1 = Rwheel(totalfitness);
offspring2 = Rwheel(totalfitness);

//crossover
crossover (offspring1,offspring2 );

// mutate

  mutate (offspring1);
  mutate (offspring2);

   c[pop]=offspring1;
  c[pop+1]=offspring2;

pop= pop+2;
}while(pop<=population);

No_of_genration=No_of_genration+1;

 if (No_of_genration> Max_No_Generation )

}

// All functions

// assign fitness
public  double chromo_fitness(double value)
{

fitness[j]= 1/Math.abs((target-chromoValue[j]));
if (target==chromoValue[j])
fitness[j]=100.0;
return fitness[j];
}

public  double chromo_Value( String chromo)
{
 //split each chromosome population into genes
int geneNo=1;
 for (i=0; i<chromLength;i+=geneLength)
  {
 // System.out.println ("c[j]"+ c[j] );

       g[geneNo]=c[j].substring(i,geneLength+i);

//System.out.print ("  g"+geneNo+"="+ g[geneNo]);

  geneNo=geneNo+1;
  }
System.out.println();
// convert each gene from binary to decimal integer

 for(geneNo=1;geneNo<=(chromLength/geneLength); geneNo++)
   {
 int temp=0,sum=0;

 for(k=0;k<geneLength;k++)
        {

 if (g[geneNo].charAt(k)=='1')
   {
      temp=(int)Math.pow(2,(geneLength-(k+1)));

   }
 else
   {
      temp=0;
   }

   sum=sum +temp;

      }

   gDecimal[geneNo]=sum;
   System.out.print  ("  gDecimal"+geneNo+"="+gDecimal[geneNo]);

   }
System.out.println();
 // z=4x1 + 3x2 +6x3
 //constrains 2x1+3x2+2x3<=440,4x1+3x3<=470, 2x1+5x2<=430,

 double b0=40.787,b1=20.52 ,b2=2.0, b3=-3.75,b4=4.0, b5=-8.12,b6=-3.4675,b7=-8.615;
 double c1=21.5,d1=2.5, c2=2.1,d2=1.3, c3=6,d3=2;
 double x1, x2, x3;

   x1=(gDecimal[1]-c1)/d1; x2=(gDecimal[2]-c2)/d2; x3=(gDecimal[3]-c3)/d3;

   chromoValue[j]=b0 +b1*x1+b2*x2 + b3*x3+b4*x1*x2 +b5*x2*x3 +b6*x1*x3 + b7*x1*x2*x3;

if (gDecimal[1]>24|| gDecimal[2]<0.0||gDecimal[2]>4 || gDecimal[3]<4.0||gDecimal[3]>8.0 )
         chromoValue[j]=-500;
// System.out.println ( "chromoValue[j]=" +chromoValue[j]   );
 return chromoValue[j];

}

public  void mutate( String offspr)
{
 double randf= randf=Math.random();
// cout<<"randf"<<randf;
 if (randf<mutation_rate)
   {
 for (i=0; i<offspr.length(); i++)
   {
 char ch =offspr.charAt(i);
 if(offspr.charAt(i)=='1')
         ch='0';
 //   offspr.charAt(i) ='0'; 
 else
 //   offspr.charAt(i)='1';
          ch='1';
   }

   }
}
public  void crossover (String offspring1, String  offspring2)
{
int cross_pt ;

float randf=randf=(float)Math.random();
//cout<<"randf"<<randf;

if(randf <crossover_rate)
{
cross_pt = (int)(randf*chromLength);

String s1= offspring1.substring(0,cross_pt) + offspring2.substring(cross_pt,chromLength);
String s2= offspring2.substring(0,cross_pt) + offspring1.substring(cross_pt,chromLength);
offspring1=s1;
offspring2= s2;

}
}

public String Rwheel( float Totalfitness)
{
double randf= randf= Math.random();

double slice = (randf*Totalfitness);

float fitnessSoFar=0;
 for(j=1;j<=population; j++)
   {

   fitnessSoFar +=fitness[j];

 if(fitnessSoFar>=slice)
   {

 return c[j];}

   }
 return  "";

}

 int n,m  ;
Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);

SP_GeneticCorrosion gn = new SP_GeneticCorrosion() ;

      }                         
  }

 // start of constructor
 public SRP_NNetExcel2(int input, int hidden, int output,int n,double [][] inputs,double [][] expectedOutputs)
     {
 this.layers = new  int[] { input, hidden, output };
df = new DecimalFormat("#.0#");
 // System.out.println ("input, hidden, output"+input +hidden+output);

 /**
         * Create all neurons and connections Connections are created in the
         * neuron class
         */
 int nm= output;
 int m=input;
 // System.out.println ("layers.length="+layers.length  );

       layers[0]=input; layers[1]=hidden; layers[2]= output;

 for (int i = 0; i < layers.length; i++)
        {
 if (i == 0)
{ // input layer
 for (int j = 0; j < layers[i]; j++)
                 {
SP_Neuron neuron = new SP_Neuron();
                    inputLayer.add(neuron);
                }
              }
 else  if (i == 1)
{ // hidden layer
 for (int j = 0; j < layers[i]; j++)
                {
SP_Neuron neuron = new SP_Neuron();
                    neuron.addInConnectionsS(inputLayer);
                    neuron.addBiasConnection(bias);
                    hiddenLayer.add(neuron);
                }
              }

 else  if (i == 2)
{ // output layer
 for (int j = 0; j < layers[i]; j++)
                  {
SP_Neuron neuron = new SP_Neuron();
                  neuron.addInConnectionsS(hiddenLayer);
                    neuron.addBiasConnection(bias);
                    outputLayer.add(neuron);
                  }
              }
 else {
                System.out.println("!Error NeuralNetwork init");
                  }
        }

 // initialize random weights
 for (SP_Neuron neuron : hiddenLayer)
        {
            ArrayList<SP_Connection> connections = neuron.getAllInConnections();
 for (SP_Connection conn : connections)
             {
 double newWeight = getRandom();
                conn.setWeight(newWeight);
              }
        }

 for (SP_Neuron neuron : outputLayer)
        {
            ArrayList<SP_Connection> connections = neuron.getAllInConnections();
 for (SP_Connection conn : connections)
            {
 double newWeight = getRandom();
                conn.setWeight(newWeight);
            }
        }

 // reset id counters
        SP_Neuron.counter = 0;
        SP_Connection.counter = 0;

 if (isTrained) {
            trainedWeights();
            updateAllWeights();
        }
    }

 // random
 double getRandom()
    {
 return randomWeightMultiplier * (rand.nextDouble() * 2 - 1); // [-1;1[
    }

 /**
     *
     * @param inputs
     *            There is equally many neurons in the input layer as there are
     *            in input variables
     */
 public  void setInput(double inputs[])
    {
 // System.out.println ("inputLayer.size()="+inputLayer.size());

 for (int i = 0; i <inputLayer.size(); i++)
        {
            inputLayer.get(i).setOutput(inputs[i]);
        }
     }

 public  double[] getOutput()
     {
 double[] outputs = new  double[outputLayer.size()];
 // System.out.println ("outputLayer.size()="+outputLayer.size());

 for (int i = 0; i <outputLayer.size(); i++)
            outputs[i] = outputLayer.get(i).getOutput();
 return outputs;
    }

 /**
     * Calculate the output of the neural network based on the input The forward
     * operation
     */
 public  void activate()
     {
 for (SP_Neuron n : hiddenLayer)
            n.calculateOutput();
 for (SP_Neuron n : outputLayer)
            n.calculateOutput();
     }

 /**
     * all output propagate back
     *
     * @param expectedOutput
     *  first calculate the partial derivative of the error with
     *  respect to each of the weight leading into the output neurons
     *   bias is also updated here
     */
 public  void applyBackpropagation(double expectedOutput[])
    {

 // error check, normalize value ]0;1[
 //   System.out.println ("expectedOutput.length="+expectedOutput.length);
 for (int i = 0; i <expectedOutput.length; i++)
        {
 double d = expectedOutput[i];
 if (d < 0 || d > 1)
            {
 if (d < 0)
                    expectedOutput[i] = 0 + epsilon;
 else
                    expectedOutput[i] = 1 - epsilon;
            }
         }

 int i = 0;
 for (SP_Neuron n : outputLayer)
         {
            ArrayList<SP_Connection> connections = n.getAllInConnections();
 for (SP_Connection con : connections)
            {
 double ak = n.getOutput();
 double ai = con.leftNeuron.getOutput();
 double desiredOutput = expectedOutput[i];

 double partialDerivative = -ak * (1 - ak) * ai
                        * (desiredOutput - ak);
 double deltaWeight = -learningRate * partialDerivative;
 double newWeight = con.getWeight() + deltaWeight;
                con.setDeltaWeight(deltaWeight);
                con.setWeight(newWeight + momentum * con.getPrevDeltaWeight());
            }
            i++;
        }

 // update weights for the hidden layer
 for (SP_Neuron n : hiddenLayer) {
            ArrayList<SP_Connection> connections = n.getAllInConnections();
 for (SP_Connection con : connections) {
 double aj = n.getOutput();
 double ai = con.leftNeuron.getOutput();
 double sumKoutputs = 0;
 int j = 0;
 for (SP_Neuron out_neu : outputLayer) {
 double wjk = out_neu.getConnection(n.id).getWeight();
 double desiredOutput = (double) expectedOutput[j];
 double ak = out_neu.getOutput();
                    j++;
                    sumKoutputs = sumKoutputs
                            + (-(desiredOutput - ak) * ak * (1 - ak) * wjk);
                }

 double partialDerivative = aj * (1 - aj) * ai * sumKoutputs;
 double deltaWeight = -learningRate * partialDerivative;
 double newWeight = con.getWeight() + deltaWeight;
                con.setDeltaWeight(deltaWeight);
                con.setWeight(newWeight + momentum * con.getPrevDeltaWeight());
            }
        }
    }
// Run NN with all feeding data plus min & max of original input output
// to convert back the result to non dimension form i e real number
 void run(int m,int hdn,int nm,int n,double [][]inputs, double [][] expectedOutputs,double[]ixmin,double[]ixmax,double[]oxmin, double[]oxmax, int maxSteps, double minError)
    {
 int i;
 double resultOutputs[ ][]= new double[n][nm];
 for (i=0; i<n; i++)
{ for (int j=0; j<nm; j++)
        { resultOutputs[i][j]=-1;}}

 // Train neural network until minError reached or maxSteps exceeded
 double error = 1;
 for (i = 0; i < maxSteps && error > minError; i++)
         {
 //  System.out.println("inputs.length= "+inputs.length);
            error = 0;
 for (int p = 0; p <n; p++)
            {
                setInput(inputs[p]);

                activate();

                output = getOutput();
                resultOutputs[p] = output;

 //   System.out.println("expectedOutputs[p].length= "+expectedOutputs[p].length);

 for (int j = 0; j < nm; j++)
                 {
 // System.out.println( "out, expec "+output[j]+expectedOutputs[p][j]);

 double err = Math.pow((output[j] - expectedOutputs[p][j]), 2);
                    error += err;
                }

                applyBackpropagation(expectedOutputs[p]);
            }
        }

 // printResult();
 // System.out.println (" printResult()inputs.length= "+inputs.length);
 double arr[][] = new  double [n][m]; // Read input
 double arro[][]= new  double [n][nm];// Read actual output

 for (int p = 0; p < n; p++)
        {
            System.out.print("INPUTS: ");

            layers[0]=m; layers[2]=nm;

 for (int x = 0; x < layers[0]; x++)
            {
System.out.print(inputs[p][x] + " ");
                arr[p][x]=inputs[p][x];
            }

            System.out.print("EXPECTED: ");
 for (int x = 0; x < layers[2]; x++)
             {
System.out.print(expectedOutputs[p][x] + " ");
            }

            System.out.print("ACTUAL: ");
 for (int x = 0; x < layers[2]; x++)
            {
System.out.print(resultOutputs[p][x] + " ");
                  arro[p][x]=resultOutputs[p][x];
            }
            System.out.println();
        }
 // call the class to convert back to number , the dimensionless result

SRP_Mapminmax_pack  mp = new SRP_Mapminmax_pack() ;
 // max & min of original non dimenisionless data  to be given
 // i-input o-output               

 double  MMR [][] =mp.dataMMR  ( n, m,arr,ixmin,ixmax );

 double  MMR2 [][] =mp.dataMMR  ( n, nm,arro,oxmin,oxmax );

       System.out.println("Input and output Results after NN");
 for (int p = 0; p < n; p++)
       {
 for (int x = 0; x < layers[0]; x++)
         {
           System.out.print(" "+MMR [p][x]);
          }

 for (int x = 0; x < layers[2]; x++)
          {
           System.out.println(" "+MMR2 [p][x]);
          }

         }

        System.out.println("Sum of squared errors = " + error);
        System.out.println("##### EPOCH " + i+"\n");
 if (i == maxSteps) {
            System.out.println("!Error training try again");
} else {
            printAllWeights();
            printWeightUpdate();
        }
    }    

    String weightKey(int neuronId, int conId)
    {
 return  "N" + neuronId + "_C" + conId;
    }

 /**
     * Take from hash table and put into all weights
     */

 // update weights for the output layer
 for (SP_Neuron n : outputLayer)

 for (SP_Connection con : connections) {

 double newWeight = weightUpdate.get(key);

 double newWeight = weightUpdate.get(key);

Program 10.3.3  SP_CathodicProtection.java

import java.io.*;
import java.util.Scanner;
import java.math.*;

public  class SP_CathodicProtection
{
 double relx, sum,uavg,tol,max,q,p,r,g,k,dia,hy,temp;
 int i,j,nx,ny,nx1,ny1,length,hx,itr,y,a,l,Nodes;
 double u[][]=new  double [1000][1000];

 public SP_CathodicProtection

( double length, double dia, double hx, double y)
   {
Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);

   nx=(int)(length/hx-1);
   ny=(int)(180/y); /* y=theta angle 0,30,*/
    nx1=nx+1;
   ny1=ny+1;
   Nodes=(nx1+1)*(ny1+1);

   System.out.println(" Insert applied potentials(V)vs. Cu/CuSo4 ref.=  ");
   u[1][1]=input.nextDouble();/* u[1][1]= u at 0 deg. l=0*/
   System.out.println("Insert total C.d.(mA/sq.m)=  ");
   q=input.nextDouble();;
   System.out.println("Insert Resistivity of media(ohm cm)=  "); 
   p=input.nextDouble();
   r=dia/2;
    hy=r*y*0.0087;
 // Potentials(mV) across the pipe curvature
 for (i=1;i<=(ny1+1); i++)
   {
      u[i+1][1]=u[i][1]- hy*(q*hy/(hx+hy))*p/100000;/* To convert ohm.cm if ( u[i+1][1] < 0)
         u[i+1][1] =0;         
   }

 // Potentials(mV) along the pipe length
 for(i=1;i<=(nx1+1);i++)
   {
       u[1][i+1]=u[1][i]-hx*(q*hx/(hx+hy))*p/100000;
 if ( u[1][i+1] < 0)
         u[1][i+1] =0;

   }

// summation and average
   sum=0;
 for(i=1;i<=(ny1+1); i++)
   {
      sum=sum + u[i][1] ;
   }
 for(i=2;i<=nx1+1;i++)
   {
      sum=sum + u[1][i] ;
   }

   uavg = sum/((nx1+1)+ ny1);

 for( i=2;i<=ny1+2;i++)
   {
 for( j=2;j<=nx1+2;j++)
      {
         u[i][j]=uavg;
      }
   }

    System.out.println("Insert level of accuracy ( Like 0.1/0.01/.001 etc)=  ");
   tol=input.nextDouble();

    k=Math.pow(hx,2)+ Math.pow(hy,2) ;
     itr=1;
 //Application of Laplace equation with numerical methods
 do
   {

      max=0;
 for( i=2;i<=ny1+1;i++)
      {
 for( j=2;j<=nx1+1;j++)
          {
   temp= (hy*hy*(u[i+1][j]+u[i-1][j])+hx*hx*(u[i][j+1]+u[i][j-1]))/(2*k);
                  relx=Math.abs(temp-u[i][j]);

 if(relx>max)
              max=relx;
               u[i][j]= temp;                              
          }

      }
 if(max<tol)
 break;
    itr++; 
   }while (itr<1000);
   System.out.println("No. of iteration= "+itr);
 for(j=1;j<=(nx1+1);j++)
   {
        u[1][j+1]=(u[1][j] + u[1][j+2] + 2*u[2][j+1])/4 ;       

   }
      System.out.println(

" Potential Distribution along the length & across the circumference");
 double ll=0;
       System.out.println();
 for( j=1;j<=(nx1+1);j++)
      {
         System.out.print(" "+ll+"m");
          ll=(ll+hx);
      }
      System.out.println();
      a=0;
 for( i=2;i<=(ny1+1);i++)
      {
 //cout<<a<<setw(6);
 for( j=1;j<=(nx1+1);j++)
        {
            System.out.print(" "+(int)u[i][j]);    
        }
          System.out.println();

         a=(int)(a+y);
         }
     }

 public  static  void main(String args[])
       {
Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);

      System.out.println("Enter Length(m),diameter(m), of the pip");
 double  length = input.nextDouble();
 double  dia = input.nextDouble();

      System.out.println("insert the Node spacing along length(m)=  ");
 double hx= input.nextDouble();
    System.out.println("insert the angular node spacing across the circumference in degree =  ");
 double y= input.nextDouble();
SP_CathodicProtection cp= new SP_CathodicProtection(length,dia,hx,y);

     }
}

11.2.1 SP_PhaseDiagram.java

import java.io.*;
import java.util.Scanner;
import java.math.*;

public  class SP_PhaseDiagram
{

double Ha,Hb,HHm1,GG1,T;
double Xb,Xbb,XXb1,XXa1,Tx,Ta,Tb,e1,e2;
double  XXb2,XXa2,HHm2,GG2,Xbs,XbL;

double G1[]=new  double[150];
double G2[]=new  double[150];
double Hm1[]=new  double[150];
double Hm2[]=new  double[150];
double Xa1[]=new  double[150];
double Xb1[]=new  double[150];
double Xa2[]=new  double[150];
double Xb2[]=new  double[150];
double s1[]=new  double[150];
double s2[]=new  double[150];
char ch;
int i,j;
double dy=0.1;double R= 0.00821; double dx= 0.005;

public SP_PhaseDiagram( double Ta, double Tb, double Ha, double Hb)
   {
Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);
      System.out.println("For Regular solution coefficient of Heat of mixing for solide e1=6.5");
   e1= input.nextDouble();
   System.out.println("For Regular solution coefficient of Heat of mixingfor solid e2=3.5");
   e2= input.nextDouble();

    T= Ta+50;// start mept A + 50

 do
   { 
 double Xbb1=0.0;

   i=0;
 do{
   Xbb1=  Xbb1+ dx;
    i=i+1;
    G1[i]=free_energy( Xbb1, T);
   Xb1[i]=Xbb1;

} while (Xbb1< 0.98);

double Xbb2=0.0;
j=0;
do {
   Xbb2=  Xbb2+ dx;
   j=j+1 ;
    G2[j]=free_energy_L( Xbb2,T);
   Xb2[j]=Xbb2;

} while (Xbb2< 0.98);

  s1=slope_S(G1);
   s2=slope_L(G2);
    compare(s1,s2);
   System.out.println("T= "+T+"\tXbs="+Xbs+"\tXbL="+XbL);

T=T+50;
} while ( T<=Tb-50);// End at mpt B minus 50
   }

public  double free_energy(double XXb1, double T )
{
    XXa1=1-XXb1;

   HHm1=e1*XXa1*XXb1;  

   GG1=(HHm1-XXb1*Hb*(1-T/Tb) + R*T*(XXa1*Math.log(XXa1)+XXb1*Math.log(XXb1)));

 return(GG1);

}
public double free_energy_L(double XXb2, double T)
{
    XXa2=1-XXb2;

   HHm2=e2*XXa2*XXb2;  

   GG2=(HHm2+XXa2*Ha*(1-T/Ta) + R*T*(XXa2*Math.log(XXa2)+XXb2*Math.log(XXb2)));

 return(GG2);

}

public  double []slope_S(double G1[])

  {
 for (i=1;i<G1.length; i++)
     s1[i]= ((G1[i+1]) -(G1[i]))/( dx);       

 return (s1);
  }

 public  double []slope_L(double G2[])
  {

 for (i=1;i<G2.length; i++)
     s2[i]= ((G2[i+1]) -(G2[i]))/( dx);

 return (s2);
  }

public  double[] compare(double s1[],double s2[])
  {
 double mini ;
   mini=Math.abs(s1[1]-s2[1]);
    i=0;
   j=0;
 do{
         i=i+1;
 do{
           j=j+1;
 if (Math.abs(s1[i]-s2[j])<mini)

         {  
           mini=Math.abs(s1[i]-s2[j]);

            Xbs=Xb1[i];
            XbL=Xb2[j];

         }

         Xb=Xb2[j];

      }while(Xb<0.9);

     Xbb=Xb1[i];

   }while(Xbb<0.9);

 double A[]= { Xbs, XbL};
 //double A[]= new double [2];
 // A[0]= Xbs; A[1]=XbL;

 return (A);
}

 public  static  void main(String args[])
       {
Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);

         System.out.println("Melting point of lower mpt metal A & then mpt of higher mpt metal B (deg.K)=");
 double  Ta = input.nextDouble();
 double  Tb = input.nextDouble();

      System.out.println("insert Enthaply of Fusion A & B (Kj/mole)=  ");
 double Ha= input.nextDouble();
 double Hb= input.nextDouble();
    Ta=1685;
   Tb=1210.4;
   Ha=50.5;
   Hb=36.94;

SP_PhaseDiagram ph= new SP_PhaseDiagram(Ta,Tb,Ha,Hb);

     }
}

%read excell data input and out from the the files 'inpf2.xlsx'& optf2.xlsx

p= [xlsread('inpf2.xlsx')] ;

t=[xlsread('outf2.xlsx')] ;

% input and target values are converted to [-1,1] vectors to p2 & t2 by the

% following function , so their values similar to dimensiinless fractiion

[p2,ps] = mapminmax(p);

[t2,ts] = mapminmax(t);

% Now divide the full data into training, validation, and test sets. 20% of the data for the validation set and

% 20% for the test set, leaving 60% for the training set. Pick the sets randomly from the original data.

%All this is accomplished with the function dividevec.[trainV,val,test] = dividevec(p2,t2,0.20,0.20);

[trainV,val,test] = dividevec(p2,t2,0.20,0.20);

% Generate nn with feedforward backpropagation fn newff , with two layers,

% ist layer(hidden) 20 neurins witgh transfer fn tansig & output layer

% one neuron with transfer fn purelin & training fn traingd

%net=newff(minmax(p2),[20,1],{'tansig','purelin'},'traingd');

net=newff(minmax(p2),[20,1],{'tansig','purelin'},'trainlm');

% Now one may chnage default parameters epoch no per show, learning rate Ir

% total epoch no, error goal etc.

net.trainParam.show = 50;

net.trainParam.lr = 0.05 ;

net.trainParam.epochs = 300;

net.trainParam.goal = 1e-5;

a2 = sim(net,p2);

Program pack 12.1 SP_MaxMin.java

 package sp_package;

 import java.util.Scanner;
 import java.io.*;
 import java.math.*;
 import java.util.*;

 public  class SP_MaxMin
   { 
 // Max value
 public  static  double getMax(double[]x)
  {
 double maxValue = x[0];
 for(int i=1;i < x.length;i++)
    {
 if(x[i] > maxValue){
         maxValue = x[i];
      }
    }
 return maxValue;
  }

// Min value
 public  static  double getMin(double[] x)
  {
 double minValue = x[0];
 for(int i=1;i<x.length;i++)
    {
 if(x[i] < minValue){
        minValue = x[i];
      }
    }
 return minValue;
  }      
}

Program pack 12.2 SP_Mapminmax_pack.java

package sp_package;

 import java.util.Scanner;
 import java.io.*;
 import java.math.*;
 import java.util.*;

 public  class SP_Mapminmax_pack
   {

 //public static void main(String args[])
 // MapMiniMax with a given data set
 public  double [][] dataMMX ( int n, int m, double arr[][])
    {

int i=0; int j=0;

doubleMMX[][]= new  double[n][m];

 double array[][] = new  double[n][m];

 for ( i=0;i<n; i++)
      {
 for( j=0;j<m; j++)
         {
         array[i][j]=arr[i][j];
         }

       }

 double x[]= new  double[n];

 for ( j=0;j<m; j++)
         {
 for( i=0; i<n; i++)
           {
            x[i]=array[i][j];
           }

 double xmax = getMax(x);
 double xmin = getMin(x);
 double y[]=getMinMax(x);

 for( i=0; i<n; i++)
           {
            MMX[i][j]= y[i];
            }
         }

 for ( i=0;i<n; i++)
      {
 for( j=0;j<m; j++)
         {
         System.out.print ("  "+ MMX[i][j] );
         }
          System.out.println();

       }

 return MMX;

     }
 // Reverse Minimax with agiven data set
 public  double [][] dataMMR ( int n, int m, double ar[][])
    {

 int i=0; int j=0;

doubleMMR[][]= new  double[n][m];

 double array[][] = new  double[n][m];

 for ( i=0;i<n; i++)
      {
 for( j=0;j<m; j++)
         {
         array[i][j]=ar[i][j];
         }

       }

 double y[]= new  double[n];
 for ( j=0; j<m; j++)
         {
 for ( i=0;i<n; i++)
            {
            y[i]=ar[i][j];
           }
 //  double max = getMax(y);
 //  double min = getMin(y);
        System.out.println("Reverse Minimax: put min and max of X1, X2 each input data range original before converting dimensionless");
 double x[]=reverseMinMax(y);

 for( i=0;i<n; i++)
           {
            MMR[i][j]= x[i];
            }
         }

System.out.println( "Reverse Minimax Data");  
 for ( i=0;i<n; i++)
      {
 for( j=0;j<m; j++)
         {
         System.out.print ("  "+ MMR[i][j] );
         }
         System.out.println();
       }

 return MMR;

     }
// Max value
 public  static  double getMax(double[]x)
  {
 double maxValue = x[0];
 for(int i=1;i < x.length;i++)
    {
 if(x[i] > maxValue){
         maxValue = x[i];
      }
    }
 return maxValue;
  }

// Min value
 public  static  double getMin(double[] x)
  {
 double minValue = x[0];
 for(int i=1;i<x.length;i++)
    {
 if(x[i] < minValue){
        minValue = x[i];
      }
    }
 return minValue;
  }
 // Method for minmax
 public  static  double [] getMinMax(double[] x)
  {
 double xmax = getMax(x);
 double xmin = getMin(x);
 double ymax=1; double ymin=-1 ;
 double y[]= new  double[x.length];
 for(int i=0;i < x.length;i++)
    {
       y[i] = (ymax-ymin)*(x[i]-xmin)/(xmax-xmin) + ymin;
    }

 return y;
  }
 // Method for reverse minmax
 public  static  double [] reverseMinMax(double[] y)
  {  
Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);
System.out.println( "Enter the min and max of the vatiable in range");

 double xmin = input.nextDouble();
 double xmax = input.nextDouble();

 double ymax=1; double ymin=-1 ;
 //  double  y[]= new double[6];
 double x[]= new  double[y.length];

 for(int i=0;i < x.length;i++)
    {
 // y[i] = (ymax-ymin)*(array[i]-xmin)/(xmax-xmin) + ymin;
       x[i]=  (xmax-xmin)*(y[i]-ymin)/(ymax-ymin)+xmin;

    }           
 return x;
  }
 public  static  void main(String args[])
  {
SP_Mapminmax_pack  mp = new SP_Mapminmax_pack() ;

Scanner input = new Scanner(System.in);
         System.out.println (" Enter no of input layer,   No of output");
 int m=input.nextInt();

 int nm =input.nextInt();

        System.out.println ("Enter No of set of data of Input & corresponding ouput");

 int n=input.nextInt();

 double arr[][] = new  double [n][m];
 double arro[][]= new  double [n][nm];

 for (int i=0; i<n; i++)
    {
      System.out.println(" Enter the inputs data X1, X2, X3 if Y=f(X1,X2,X3) " );
 for (int j=0; j<m; j++)
       {
       arr [i][j]=input.nextDouble();
       }
       System.out.println(" Enter the corresponding output Y" );

 for (int j=0; j<nm; j++)
       {
       arro [i][j]=input.nextDouble();
       }
       System.out.println(" Next set of data");
     }

      System.out.println ("arr." +arr.length);
        System.out.println ("arr[2]." +arr[2].length);

 //  double MMX [][]= new double [n][m];
 double  MMX [][] =mp.dataMMX (   n,   m,  arr );// input
 double  MMX1 [][] =mp.dataMMX (   n,   nm,  arro );// output

  System.out.println ("Input in dimensionless frmat" );

 for (int i=0;i<n; i++)
      {
 for(int j=0;j<m; j++)
         {
         System.out.print ("  "+(float)MMX[i][j] );
         }
         System.out.println();
       }     

    System.out.println ("output in dimensionless frmat" );

 for (int i=0;i<n; i++)
      {
 for(int j=0;j<nm; j++)
         {
         System.out.print ("  "+(float)MMX1[i][j] );
         }
         System.out.println();
       }

 double  MMR [][] =mp.dataMMR  ( n, m,MMX );

     System.out.println("Reverse Minimax Data");  

 for (int i=0;i<n; i++)
      {
 for(int j=0;j<m; j++)
         {
         System.out.print ("  "+(float)MMR[i][j] );
         }
         System.out.println();
       }

  }    
}

Font font = new Font("Arial", Font.BOLD,16);

     );

Program pack 12.4 SRP_Graph.java

package sp_package;
import java.awt.*;
import java.awt.event.*;

public  class SRP_Graph extends Frame {

 private String xAxisTitle, yAxisTitle, graphTitle;
 private  boolean keys;

 public  void Graph () {
//-------------
 /* the simple constructor -
       leaves the window and axes titles blank */
    initializeGraph();
xAxisTitle = "";
yAxisTitle = "";
graphTitle = "";
  }

 public  void Graph (String g, String x, String y) {
//------------
 /* the alternative constructor -
       has window and axes titles */
    initializeGraph();
    graphTitle = g;
    xAxisTitle = x;
    yAxisTitle = y;
  }

 private  class Dataset {
//---------------------
 /* Simple class for recording basic info
  about a set of points.  Allows more than one
  graph to be drawn on an axis.
  All initialisation done in nextGraph. */
 int count;
 int plotType;
    String title;
 boolean colorRequired, symbolRequired, titleRequired, lineRequired;
  }

 private List points;
 private List datasets;

 private  void initializeGraph () {
datasets = new List ();
    nextGraph();
points = new List ();
    xMax = yMax = Double.MIN_VALUE;
    xMin = yMin = Double.MAX_VALUE;
keys = false;
    setSize (640,480);
 super.setTitle ("Graph");
    addWindowListener (new WindowAdapter () {
 public  void windowClosing(WindowEvent e) {
        dispose();
      }
    });
  }

 public  void nextGraph () {
//---------------------
Dataset d = new Dataset ();
    d.count = 0;
 // d.plotType = black;
d.title = "";
d.symbolRequired = false;
d.colorRequired = false;
d.titleRequired = false;
d.lineRequired = true;
    datasets.add (d);
  }
 public  void setLine (boolean b) {
//-------------------
    ((Dataset) datasets.current()).lineRequired = b;
 if (b==false) 
((Dataset) datasets.current()).symbolRequired = true;
  }

 public  void add (double x, double y) {
//---------------
Point points  = new Point (x,y);
 // points.Point (x,y)); 
    ((Dataset)datasets.current()).count++;
 if (x > xMax) xMax = x;
 if (x < xMin) xMin = x;
 if (y > yMax) yMax = y;
 if (y < yMin) yMin = y;
  }

 public  void showGraph () {
//---------------------
    repaint();
    setVisible(true);
  }

private  class Point {
//------------------
 /* simple class for an x,y coordinate */
 double xCoord, yCoord;
    Point (double x, double y)
     {
      xCoord = x;
      yCoord = y;
    }
  }

 private  double xSpread, ySpread, xMin, xMax, yMin, yMax;
 private  int xAxisLength, yAxisLength, xOrigin, yOrigin;
 private  int xBorder, yBorder;

 public  void paint (Graphics g) {
//-----------------
 // calulate length of axes from window size minus a border of 20
    xBorder = 40;
    yBorder = 80;

    xAxisLength = (int) getWidth() - 2*xBorder;
    yAxisLength = (int) getHeight() - 2*yBorder;

 // calculate value spreads from mins and maxs which have
 // been recorded as we go
    xSpread = xMax - xMin;
    ySpread = yMax - yMin;
 if (xMin > 0) xOrigin = scaleX(xMin); else xOrigin = scaleX(0);
 if (yMin > 0) yOrigin = scaleY(yMin); else yOrigin = scaleY(0);

 //  drawAxes(g);
    plotGraphs(g);
  }

 private  int scaleX(double x) {
//------------------
 return (int) ((x-xMin) / xSpread*xAxisLength)  + xBorder ;
  }

 private  int scaleY(double y) {
//------------------
 return (int) (yAxisLength - ((y-yMin) /
                  ySpread*yAxisLength)) + yBorder;
  }

 private  void plotGraphs (Graphics g) {
//-----------------------
    Point p, q;
 int x1, y1, x2, y2;
    Dataset d;
 boolean lastset;
    Color c;

 // Loop through each dataset
    datasets.reset();
 /* The points are in one big list, split by the
    counts recorded in each dataset */
    points.reset();
    lastset = datasets.eol();

 do {
      d = (Dataset) datasets.current();
          p = (Point) points.current();
      x1 = scaleX(p.xCoord);
      y1 = scaleY(p.yCoord);
 if (d.symbolRequired)

 // Loop through the points as stored in the list
 for (int i=1; i<d.count; i++) {
        points.succ();
        q = (Point) points.current();
        x2 = scaleX(q.xCoord);
        y2 = scaleY(q.yCoord);

 // plot the line and/or point
 if (d.lineRequired)
          g.drawLine(x1, y1, x2, y2);
 if (d.symbolRequired)
           x1 = x2; y1 = y2;
      }
      lastset = datasets.eol();
 if (!lastset) {
        datasets.succ();
        points.succ();
      }
} while (!lastset);
  }     

}

public  classSRP_package_graph extends Frame

// Download Jexcelapi add jar file from the download folder by
